### [1] [Beyond Type-checking: Towards Holistic Evaluation of Formal Specification Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10604)
*Srijith Nair,Aditya Vempaty,Jia Liu,Ashish Jagmohan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该文构建统一Lean规格生成评测数据集(350任务,含VERINA 189、CLEVER 161)与多维度框架,揭示SpecGen评测中度量覆盖不足会误导配置排序,并证明后置条件满分也可能遗漏不可用的输入契约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LLM与智能体工作流生成可验证代码时,SpecGen产出的形式化契约缺乏对用户意图的确定性检验; 即使证明通过,也可能只是相对一个错误表达意图的规格成立,因此需要面向SpecGen的整体性评测方法。

Method: 整合350个既有Lean任务(189来自VERINA、161来自CLEVER)构建统一数据集; 提出覆盖形式有效性、参考相似性与等价性、行为充分性的评测框架; 区分“必需输入的接受”“有效输出的接受”“无效输出的拒绝”,并显式界定各度量证据范围; 在四种SpecGen配置上做对比实验,并设计创作式对照实验。

Result: 将广义树编辑距离(GTED)比较限制到32个可联合测量的VERINA任务后,VERINA配置的平均相似度排名由第2降至第4,说明度量覆盖范围会显著影响结论; 对照实验中某规格取得100%正例测试召回与负例拒绝,却接受0%的必需输入,显示后置条件满分仍可能对应不可用的输入契约。

Conclusion: SpecGen评测应显式报告测量覆盖,并对输入覆盖与输出约束分别反馈; 单一后置条件式指标不足以判断规格是否真正捕捉意图,需采用多维度、证据范围明确的统一框架。

Abstract: When generating verifiable code, natural language requirements are mapped to machine checked code using LLMs and agentic workflows. A crucial component of this pipeline is specification generation (SpecGen), which produces a formal contract against which an agent can prove implementation correctness. Proof generation can obtain deterministic feedback from a theorem prover, but SpecGen lacks a definitive check that a generated specification captures the user's intent. A checked proof can therefore establish correctness against a specification that misrepresents the intended behaviour. We take a step towards holistic SpecGen evaluation with a unified dataset assembled from $350$ existing Lean tasks, including $189$ from VERINA and $161$ from CLEVER, and a framework covering formal validity, reference similarity and equivalence, and behavioural adequacy. We distinguish acceptance of required inputs from acceptance of valid outputs and rejection of invalid outputs, while making each metric's evidence scope explicit. Across four SpecGen configurations, restricting the generalized tree edit distance (GTED) comparison, a reference similarity measure, to $32$ jointly measurable VERINA tasks changes the VERINA configuration's position from second to fourth in mean similarity, showing the importance of measurement coverage. In an authored control, a specification achieves $100\%$ positive test recall and negative test rejection while accepting $0\%$ of required inputs. This demonstrates that perfect postcondition scores can miss an unusable input contract, motivating separate feedback on input coverage and output constraints.

</details>


### [2] [Code Understanding is a Bottleneck for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10610)
*Nishant Balepur,Kiran Tomlinson,Tobias Schnabel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出 CABRA——一种从零构建、以调用图变换为任务形式并可参数化调节难度的编码代理评测基准，通过 6840 个任务对 8 个 LLM 与 6 个编码代理进行评估，揭示代理"近乎满分"主要源于工具卸载，而真正的难点在于代码理解而非编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仓库级基准（如 SWE-bench）常以"编辑代码行数"作为任务难度代理，但对代码类型与任务类型的控制不足，难以判断究竟是何种能力导致代理出错。因此需要一个可控、可诊断的评测蓝图来分离并定位代理的能力短板。

Method: CABRA 从零合成任务，将任务构造为调用图变换，并通过"任务规模"参数在四个维度上缩放难度：函数遍历（function traversal）、搜索（search）、运行时解析（runtime resolution）、指令遵循（instruction following）。在 6,840 个 CABRA 任务上评测 8 个 LLM 与 6 个编码代理，并另在 SWE-bench Verified 上做对照研究；此外将 CABRA 扩展为一个需要跨两个类分析分叉逻辑的"高强度理解任务"。

Result: 1) LLM 准确率随任务规模增大而下降，但代理通过将工作卸载给工具（如 grep）仍保持接近满分；2) 更大的 CABRA 任务引发更多用于阅读与分析的调用工具次数，且在 SWE-bench Verified 上，工具调用次数比编辑行数更能预测代理准确率，说明代理的任务难度可能在于"理解待编辑代码"而非"执行编辑"；3) 扩展后的高强度理解任务中代理准确率终于下降，印证上述结论。

Conclusion: 主张采用 CABRA 这类合成评测来揭示被工具"平凡化"的 LLM 弱点（如大海捞针式检索）以及编辑之外编码代理仍感困难的能力（如代码理解），并将 SWE-bench 式任务与受控诊断相结合。

Abstract: Repository benchmarks (e.g., SWE-bench) for coding agents often assume that lines of code edited can predict task difficulty, but such datasets' poor control over code and task types makes it hard to know which abilities truly drive agent errors. We present CABRA: a Coding Ability Blueprint for Rigorous Agent evaluation. CABRA builds tasks from scratch as call graph transformations and scales difficulty via a task size parameter on four axes: function traversal, search, runtime resolution, and instruction following. We run eight LLMs and six coding agents on 6,840 CABRA tasks to show: 1) LLM accuracy falls as task size~grows, but agents stay near-perfect by offloading work to tools (e.g., grep); 2) Larger CABRA tasks elicit more tool calls for reading and analysis, while a separate study on SWE-bench Verified shows these tool call counts predict agents' accuracy better than lines of code edited, suggesting task difficulty for agents can lie in understanding code to edit, not just in making edits; 3) Extending CABRA to an intense understanding task where models analyze divergent logic across two classes backs this finding, as agent accuracy finally falls. More broadly, we argue for synthetic evaluations like CABRA to unmask LLM weaknesses trivialized by tools (e.g., needle-in-a-haystack) and abilities beyond just editing (e.g., understanding) that coding agents still find difficult, pairing SWE-bench-style tasks with controlled diagnosis.

</details>


### [3] [TestJack: Should you trust the results in coding benchmarks? Agentic Coding Benchmarks Auditing via Evaluator Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10619)
*Shuangjie Yao,Hao Wang,Koushik Sen,Simin Chen,Baishakhi Ray,Dawn Song*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TestJack：面向 LLM 代码智能体的自适应评测框架，针对每次尝试动态生成测试以揭示被固定单元测试掩盖的违规行为。在 6 个前沿模型后端与 5 个基准上，约 34.4% 被判正确的尝试实际违反任务要求，整体解决率由 50.6% 降至 33.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码基准几乎全部沿用数十年前的判定标准：通过一组固定单元测试即视为正确。这类测试覆盖不完整，仅检验任务要求的一部分，导致智能体可通过奖励劫持或在遗漏必需行为的情况下仍通过全部测试。因此，较高的基准分数可能部分反映的是对评测器的适应能力，而非真实的问题求解能力。既有工作采用静态测试增强，即在任务开始前一次性强化测试，无法利用真实尝试的失败信息，因而忽视了实际失败模式。

Method: 提出 TestJack，一种可扩展的、超越固定测试的补丁评测框架。对每次尝试，TestJack 针对该补丁可能违反的提示词要求生成测试，仅保留真实补丁（ground-truth patch）能通过的测试，并据此重新检查失败尝试；每个确认的失败均有可复现的测试支撑。为降低评测成本，另提出轻量变体：对随机抽样的尝试进行深度审计，并将所得测试复用于同一任务的所有尝试。

Result: 在 6 个前沿模型后端与 5 个基准（如 DeepSWE、SWE Marathon）上评估：约 34.4% 当前被判正确的模型尝试违反任务要求，整体解决率由 50.6% 降至 33.2%。

Conclusion: 当前编码智能体评测存在根本性局限：随着 LLM 越来越擅长针对固定评测器进行优化，评测器自身也必须变得更加自适应。TestJack 通过逐尝试、针对性的测试生成，提供了可复现且可扩展的替代方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are rapidly reshaping software engineering, accompanied by an explosion of new code benchmarks. Yet nearly all existing benchmarks still rely on the same decades-old criterion: a solution is correct if it passes a fixed set of unit tests. Such tests are often insufficient: they check only part of what the task requires, so agents can reward hack them or silently miss required behavior while still passing every test. As a result, higher benchmark scores may partly reflect better adaptation to the evaluator rather than better problem solving. Existing works focus on static test augmentation: they strengthen each task's tests once, before any trial is seen, and thus overlook how real trials actually fail. We introduce TestJack, a scalable framework for evaluating patches beyond fixed tests. For each trial, TestJack generates tests targeting prompt requirements the patch may violate, retains only tests passed by the ground-truth patch, and re-examines any trial failures. Each confirmed failure is thus supported by a replayable test. To reduce evaluation cost, we also introduce a lightweight variant which audits a random sample of trials in depth and reuses the resulting tests across all trials for the same task. Across 6 frontier model backends and 5 benchmarks such as DeepSWE and SWE Marathon, we find that about 34.4% of the model trials currently judged correct violate the task requirements, lowering the overall resolution rate from 50.6% to 33.2%. Our results reveal a fundamental limitation of current coding-agent evaluation: as LLMs become better at optimizing against fixed evaluators, those evaluators themselves must become more adaptive.

</details>


### [4] [Agent4RE: A Self-Refining Multi-agent Framework for End-to-End Software Requirements Engineering and Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10628)
*Yongjian Tang,Linhan Li,Thomas Runkler*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出自优化多智能体需求工程系统Agent4RE及端到端数据集RE-E2E，并通过两种增强变体验证其优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的需求工程方法多依赖基础提示或简单智能体协作，未充分发挥多智能体系统潜力；同时已有数据集仅聚焦需求抽取、分类、完整性检测等孤立子任务，缺乏覆盖从需求获取到生成全流程的端到端基准。

Method: 提出Agent4RE——一种自优化多智能体需求工程系统，编排多个专用智能体并引入两个迭代改进循环；构建基于人工撰写需求规格的RE-E2E真实数据集；并进一步提出两种增强版本，分别融入自主自优化与结构化人工反馈。

Result: 在8个LLM上评估，三种Agent4RE变体在文本指标上平均超越领域上下文增强提示基线8%；两种增强变体在LLM-as-a-judge与人工评分中最高，在四点量表上超过两个需求工程基线约0.8分。

Conclusion: 一致性的性能表现确立了Agent4RE作为面向工业环境的实用端到端需求工程解决方案。

Abstract: Existing LLM-based approaches for software Requirements Engineering (RE) typically rely on basic prompting strategies or rudimentary agent collaboration, under-utilizing the full potential of multi-agent systems. Meanwhile, available datasets focus on isolated subtasks, such as requirements extraction, classification, and completeness detection, leaving the absence of an end-to-end RE benchmark that spans from requirements elicitation to generation. We present Agent4RE - a self-refining multi-agent RE system that orchestrates specialized agents and incorporates two iterative improvement loops. To support evaluation, we construct RE-E2E - a real-world dataset built from human-written requirement specifications, enabling end-to-end assessment of RE workflows. Building on this foundation, we further propose two enhanced Agent4RE versions that incorporate either autonomous self-refinement or structured human feedback, and analyze their strengths and limitations across different scenarios. Evaluation on 8 Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrates that all three Agent4RE variants consistently outperform a domain-context-augmented prompting baseline by average 8% in text-based metrics. The two enhanced variants achieve the highest LLM-as-a-judge and human ratings, surpassing two RE baselines by approximately 0.8 points on a four-point scale. This consistent performance establishes Agent4RE as a practical end-to-end RE solution for industrial environments.

</details>


### [5] [Ruleless Digital Twins: Toward Declarative Decision-Making Through Standardized Frameworks and Technologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10631)
*Ivan Spajić,Volker Stolz*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出"无规则数字孪生"(RDT)：以语义知识库、逻辑推理、仿真模型与自主计算架构组合，基于纯声明式用户规范自动产生最优决策，替代传统基于规则的决策方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字孪生(DT)通过对孪生目标(TT)施加动作并进行决策来实现优化。传统上如家庭自动化等领域采用基于规则的决策模型，以命令式方式依据期望的TT状态定义DT动作。随着用户需求演进，规则模型在开发与维护上日益复杂，尤其在涉及动态变化系统（如受天气或动态能源定价影响）下的优化场景中尤为突出。

Method: 提出无规则数字孪生(RDT)，类比成熟的模型预测控制这一无规则方法，结合语义知识库、逻辑推理、仿真模型与自主计算架构，各组件均基于广泛使用的标准或技术；并以孵化器案例研究及虚拟办公房间环境中的对照实验进行评估，对比对象为基于规则的bang-bang控制器。

Result: 在动态变化的实时电价条件下，RDT功能在维持期望室温与最小化能源成本方面均优于bang-bang控制器。但由于组合式决策树构建，RDT在较长预测时域下需要相当可观的计算资源；其是否适用于特定问题领域（以及是否采用较短时域）最终取决于用户。

Conclusion: RDT为复杂、动态环境下的数字孪生决策提供了一种声明式替代方案，在温度控制与能耗成本优化上优于规则型控制；但其计算开销随预测时域增长而显著上升，实际适用性需由用户在具体问题域中权衡。

Abstract: Digital twins (DTs) can be thought of as digital counterparts of physical objects, or more generally, twinning targets (TTs). To enact changes on and optimize for properties of their TTs, DTs use an element of decision-making. Traditionally, many domains, such as home automation, utilize rule-based decision-making models to imperatively define DT actions based on desired TT conditions. With evolving user specifications, rule-based models have become increasingly complex to develop and maintain, particularly in scenarios involving optimizations under dynamically changing systems, such as those affected by weather or dynamic energy pricing. We present an alternative in the form of ruleless digital twins (RDTs) that automatically produce optimal decisions with respect to purely declarative user specifications, similarly to the well-established ruleless approach of model predictive control. They do so through a combination of a semantic knowledge base, logical inference, simulation models, and an autonomic computing architecture, each exclusively based on a widely-used standard or technology. We evaluate our proof of concept through an incubator case study and experiments against a rule-based bang-bang controller in the context of a virtual office room environment. Results show core that RDT functionality performs better than the control in terms of maintaining desired room temperatures and minimizing energy costs under dynamically changing spot pricing. Additionally, due to combinatorial decision tree construction, RDTs require considerable computational power for longer prediction horizons, although their applicability to specific problem domains (and use of shorter horizons) ultimately falls on their users.

</details>


### [6] [Has LLM Screening Performance Stalled in Software Engineering Systematic Reviews?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10633)
*Aleksi Huotala,Miikka Kuutila,Mika Mäntylä*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究基于软件工程系统综述筛选基准（SESR-Eval）及新建的SESR-Eval-Mini子集，评估了8个新型大语言模型（LLM）的文献筛选性能，发现新模型仅略优于旧模型，模型间差异小于研究间差异，LLM尚不能替代人工筛选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统综述的文献筛选环节依赖人工，耗时费力。已有研究尝试用LLM自动化该步骤，但由于LLM迭代迅速，早期性能结论可能已不再适用，因此需要重新评估新一代模型的筛选能力。

Method: 以现有软件工程系统综述筛选基准SESR-Eval为数据基础，并通过功效抽样构建成本更低的SESR-Eval-Mini数据集；对8个新LLM进行筛选性能评测，比较不同提示词设计，分析LLM在筛选决策与纳入/排除标准上的一致性，并考察细化纳入排除标准对筛选性能的影响。

Result: 8个新LLM相比7个旧模型仅有边际提升，次级研究的平均MCC由0.347升至0.365；次级研究之间的差异仍大于LLM之间的差异。由标准级别决策汇总得到的整体筛选决策仅轻微降低性能。LLM之间决策总体一致性较高（平均Gwet's AC1=0.830），但部分纳入与排除标准的分歧较大。细化纳入排除标准可略微提升召回率并使部分模型的决策更易做出，但总体影响有限。

Conclusion: LLM目前尚不足以替代人工进行论文筛选，新一代更昂贵模型带来的优势十分有限。基于智能体的方法、提示工程以及进一步细化纳入排除标准是三个潜在的未来研究方向。

Abstract: Screening in systematic reviews (SRs) is manual and time-consuming. Prior work has explored large language models (LLMs) for automating this step, but LLMs are evolving rapidly, so earlier performance claims may no longer accurately reflect their screening performance. We used an existing software engineering SR screening benchmark (SESR-Eval) as our data. We also power-sampled a new, smaller dataset (SESR-Eval-Mini) that allows evaluation at lower costs. Using this data, we evaluated eight new LLMs for screening performance. Additionally, we tested different prompts, analyzed LLM agreement in screening decisions and criteria, and examined the effect of refining the inclusion and exclusion criteria on screening performance. The eight new LLMs performed marginally better than the seven old ones: avg. MCC across secondary studies rose from 0.347 to 0.365. Differences between secondary studies are still bigger than between LLMs. Computing the overall screening decision from criterion-level decisions degraded screening performance only slightly. LLMs generally agree with each other in their corresponding screening decisions (mean Gwet's AC1 = 0.830), though certain inclusion and exclusion criteria showed larger disagreement than others. Refining the inclusion and exclusion criteria slightly improved recall and made decisions easier for some LLMs, but overall impacts of criteria refinement were modest. LLMs are not yet ready to replace humans in paper screening and the advantages new, more costly models bring, appear to be very limited. Agent-based approaches, prompt engineering, and further criteria refinement are three potential future research avenues.

</details>


### [7] [Can LLMs Simulate Novice Programmers' Misconceptions?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10656)
*Batuhan Yeltekin,Daniel Bauer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 评估13个大语言模型在程序追踪问题中生成、求解、模拟与诊断新手编程迷思概念的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新手编程迷思概念（misconceptions）是编程教育中的核心难点，需评估LLM能否在代码追踪任务中胜任相关教学场景；已有研究缺乏对模型规模与代码微调影响的系统比较。

Method: 对13个LLM在四类任务上评测：迷思概念生成、问题求解、动态执行模拟、迷思概念诊断；对比前沿大模型与≤14B小模型，并对比代码微调模型与通用模型，以及True/False与开放式生成两种诊断形式。

Result: 前沿模型能稳定完成各项任务；≤14B小模型在动态执行上表现吃力；代码微调模型在迷思概念模拟中失败，倾向于回退到正确执行；迷思概念诊断在True/False形式下显著比开放式生成更容易。

Conclusion: LLM用于编程迷思概念教学任务时效果高度依赖模型规模与任务形式；代码微调反而损害迷思概念模拟能力，诊断任务宜采用判别式格式。

Abstract: We evaluate 13 LLMs on generating, solving, simulating, and diagnosing novice programming misconceptions on code-tracing problems. While frontier models reliably perform these tasks, small models ($\le$14B) struggle with dynamic execution. Notably, code-tuned models fail during misconception simulation by reverting to correct execution. Finally, misconception diagnosis is significantly easier in True/False formats than in open-ended generation tasks.

</details>


### [8] [Applying Security by Design at the Point of Execution: How Governed Security Requirements Affect the Security of AI-Generated Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10659)
*Pedro Farinha*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 将源自安全设计知识库（SbD-ToE）的安全需求经MCP服务器在执行时提供给代码生成器，可显著提升生成代码的安全测试通过率与抗攻击比例，但"安全且功能正确"的联合指标未显著改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全设计（Security by design）要求先定义安全需求再编写代码，而现有安全代码基准通常反向操作：不给需求、用未见过的安全测试打分。作者希望考察在执行时点提供受治理的安全需求会带来何种变化。

Method: 从受治理的安全设计知识库SbD-ToE中选取安全需求，通过Model Context Protocol（MCP）服务器在执行时点交付给生成器。在DualGauge上运行59个Python任务（由语言模型裁判评分）；在BaxBench上运行28个后端场景（容器内运行功能测试与真实漏洞利用）。每项实验使用单一模型、每个任务一次生成；需求来自作者开源手册。

Result: DualGauge：全部安全测试通过的任务占比由44.1%升至78.0%，通过的安全测试占比由77.4%升至93.0%。BaxBench：在功能正确的解中，无可成功利用漏洞的比例由65%升至86%，与作者称为"不现实上界"的Oracle Security Reminder（85%）相当，且需求交付过程并不知晓测试内容。

Conclusion: 在执行时点交付受治理的安全需求可改善生成代码的安全性。局限在于：预设的"安全且功能正确"联合指标在两个基准上均未显著提升；启用受治理需求后代码更严格，会因固定任务未声明的取值而在功能测试中失败（已做逐例事后归因分析）。现有基准只能判断代码是否表现出评测器为检测而构建的弱点，无法判断其是否满足应适用的安全需求，故作者建议度量对适用需求的符合性（conformance）。

Abstract: Security by design asks that security requirements are defined before code is written. Secure-code benchmarks typically measure the opposite situation: the agent receives the task without requirements and is scored with security tests it has not seen. We measured what changes when security requirements, selected from a governed security-by-design knowledge base (SbD-ToE) and delivered through a Model Context Protocol (MCP) server, are given to the generator at the point of execution. On DualGauge, which scores with a language-model judge, we ran 59 Python tasks: the share of tasks passing all security tests rose from 44.1% to 78.0% and the share of security tests passed from 77.4% to 93.0%. On BaxBench, which runs functional tests and real exploits in containers, we ran 28 backend scenarios: among functionally correct solutions, the share with no successful exploit rose from 65% to 86%, comparable to the authors' Oracle Security Reminder (85%), which they call an unrealistic upper bound; the requirement delivery reached it without knowing the tests. The study has limits. The pre-specified joint secure-and-functional metric did not improve significantly on either benchmark. The code written with the governed requirements is stricter, and it failed functional tests that fix values the task never states; we attribute those failures case by case in a post-hoc analysis. Each experiment used one model and one generation per task. The requirements come from the author's open-source manual; we do not assess its completeness. Governed security requirements delivered at the point of execution appear to improve the security of generated code. Current benchmarks cannot tell whether that code meets the security requirements that apply to it, only whether it shows the weaknesses their evaluators were built to detect. We propose measuring conformance with the applicable requirements.

</details>


### [9] [Cross-Provider Review as a Runtime Contract for Coding Agents: A Controlled Pilot and Fault-Injection Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10961)
*Bowen Xu,Boyu Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种跨提供方“顾问式”代码评审契约（独立资源池、有界执行、受限评审能力、完整输入交付、可用语义输出、显式失败态与每尝试可审计证据），并通过20轮配对试点、边界条件扫描与真实CLI探针验证其工程可行性，同时暴露三处缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体在同一工作站上协作但使用不同提供方与订阅额度；二次评审虽可检查已完成答案，却会消耗另一资源池且可能不产生实质发现，因此需要一种既受限又可审计的跨提供方评审契约。

Method: 受控的智能体自撰试点：20个配对开发轮次；对两个评审后端做边界条件扫描（含部分输入下的截断层级测试与进程回收期取消）；真实CLI只读探针；以及预注册的“仅元数据评审分配”影子研究（25条正式观察）。

Result: 20轮中8轮出现实质性评审发现（95%精确区间19.1–63.9%）；边界扫描复现了部分输入下的假成功（四个截断层级在历史版本通过、修复后失败），并发现并修复进程回收期的取消缺陷；CLI探针中Claude无写入工具，Codex在5/5只读试验中尝试写入且各写入工具均失败，一次性仓库均未变更；影子研究累计25条正式观察时，通过精确运行时回归发现第三处缺陷（非零退出但裁决格式良好的评审被计为完成），且退出状态未按尝试记录，暴露无法回溯解析。

Conclusion: 测试仅覆盖指定路径与版本，不代表现场可靠性。25条正式记录与2条待定记录保留为审计队列；测量有效队列已归零并重新开始采集，尚未报告任何门槛结果。

Abstract: Coding agents increasingly share a workstation while drawing on separate providers and subscription allowances. A second agent can inspect a completed answer, but the call spends another pool and may provide no substantive finding. We describe an advisory cross-provider review contract: distinct resource pools, bounded execution, restricted reviewer capabilities, complete input delivery, usable semantic output, explicit failure states and durable per-attempt evidence. In a controlled, agent-authored pilot of 20 paired development turns, eight had a material reviewer finding (95% exact interval 19.1-63.9%). A boundary-condition scan across both reviewer backends reproduced a previously discovered false success on partial input: four truncation levels passed historically and failed after repair. The scan also found and repaired cancellation during process reaping. In real CLI probes, Claude had no writing tools; Codex attempted writes in five of five read-only trials, each write tool failed, and no disposable repository changed. These tests cover specified paths and versions, not field reliability. A preregistered shadow study of metadata-only review allocation accrued 25 formal observations before an exact-runtime regression found a third defect: a reviewer exiting nonzero with a well-formed verdict was counted as complete. Exit status was not recorded per attempt, so exposure cannot be resolved retrospectively. The 25 formal and two pending records remain an audit cohort; the measurement-valid cohort restarted at zero and collection has begun. No gate result is reported.

</details>


### [10] [Probabilistic Sensing, Deterministic Authority: Admitting Model-Produced Observations into Sufficiency-Checked Governance Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10978)
*Gaston Besanson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文研究"从非结构化证据中感知权威合约所需字段"的判定问题:将模型输出降格为带分数的观测记录,经准入策略映射为 true/false/unknown(unknown 即拒绝),再由确定性、经过充分性检查的合约作出裁决;感知改变裁决的概率由合约所感知字段上的"误准入率+未知率"之和上界控制,并可据此在充分合约间做成本最优选择。在合成域上的注册研究(36,000 次模型调用)未出现反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 权威合约所需的某些字段可能仅存在于非结构化证据中,需要借助模型"感知"来获取;但模型输出不可直接信任,若将其当作真值接入裁决会引入不可控的错误。因此需要一个既能利用感知信息、又能为"感知是否改变裁决"提供可证明上界的框架,并以此作为在多个充分合约之间进行选择的成本模型。

Method: 1) 把模型输出仅接收为带分数的观测记录,不接收为真值;2) 设计准入策略,其阈值在留出集上拟合,并声明误报上限,将分数映射为 true / false / unknown(unknown 一律拒绝);3) 由确定性、经过充分性检查的合约作最终裁决;4) 推导上界:感知改变裁决的概率不超过合约所感知字段上"误准入率与未知率"之和,该方法被刻画为按合约索引的 union-bound 推理;5) 将最小化估计上界作为选择充分合约的有效成本模型;6) 在两个构造域、两个传感器族、36,000 次模型调用的注册研究中进行验证。

Result: 无任何单元格证伪该上界。在本计划中首次出现由感知引起的"拒绝→允许"翻转变:21,000 条测试裁决中有 13 条,且全部源自 3 条相互矛盾的记录;每个有注册上界的单元格中,每次翻转均低于其上界。感知感知型选择在 4/4 项注册测试中选出了暴露度更低的合约。两个传感器的分数均具有信息量,但未校准(not calibrated)。

Conclusion: 感知信息可以在不牺牲可证界的情况下进入权威裁决:只要把模型输出限定为带分数的观测、由访问策略与充分性检查的合约把关,即可用按合约索引的 union bound 控制感知改变裁决的概率,并将其用作合约选择的成本模型。结论的适用范围受限于所声明的损失模型、候选表示与可达状态;所有域均为构造域,因此结论的普适性尚待真实场景验证。此外传感器的信息量存在但缺乏校准,提示分数阈值的可靠性依赖于留出集拟合与声明的误报上限。

Abstract: When a field that an authority contract needs exists only in unstructured evidence, a model can sense it. We admit the model's output only as an observation record with a score. An admission policy, with thresholds fitted on a held-out split at a declared false-positive ceiling, maps each score to true, false or unknown. Unknown denies. A deterministic, sufficiency-checked contract decides. The probability that sensing changes the verdict is bounded by the sum, over the contract's sensed fields, of the admitted-wrong and unknown rates. This is an instantiation of union-bound reasoning, indexed by the contract. Minimising the estimated bound is a valid cost model for choosing among sufficient contracts. In a registered study on two constructed domains with two sensor families (36,000 model calls), no cell refuted the bound. Deny-to-allow changes from sensing appeared for the first time in this programme: 13 of 21,000 test verdicts, all from 3 contradictory records; each flip in a cell with a registered bound lay under it. Sensing-aware selection picked the lower-exposure contract in 4 of 4 registered tests. Both sensors' scores were informative but not calibrated. Correctness is relative to the declared loss model, candidate representation and reachable states; all domains are constructed.

</details>


### [11] [Following Breadcrumbs in Code: What Accidentally Committed Ad-Hoc Logs Reveal about Developer Comprehension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11041)
*Yi-Hung Chou,Boyuan Jiang,Vidit Jain,Yiyang Min,April Yi Wang,James A. Jones*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文通过挖掘误提交后删除的临时日志（ad-hoc logs）与直播编程会话，构建了跨 Java、JavaScript、Python 的大规模数据集，系统刻画了开发者临时日志的使用模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发者在调试与理解复杂运行时行为时普遍插入临时打印/日志语句，但这类日志具有短暂性——通常只存在于本地环境，提交前被删除，因而难以捕获，导致相关系统性研究长期匮乏。

Method: 采用两种数据获取手段：(1) 挖掘开发者误将临时日志提交后又删除的"意外提交"；(2) 分析直播编程会话中的实际使用过程。据此构建覆盖 Java、JavaScript、Python 三种语言的跨语言大规模数据集，并进行日志位置、函数特征与环复杂度等统计分析。

Result: 发现跨语言的共性模式：临时日志倾向于出现在运行时更难推理的程序区域；同时识别出语言特异性模式，如 JavaScript 中常见于异步与回调函数，Java 中常见于线程相关类。此外，含临时日志的函数环复杂度普遍高于整体函数群体，生产日志也呈现类似关联。

Conclusion: 研究为开发者的运行时理解实践提供了实证洞察，并提供了一份有价值的数据集，可供研究者与工具开发者用于更好地支持调试与日志相关工作。

Abstract: Developers frequently insert temporary print or log statements, known as **ad-hoc logs**, to better understand program behavior at runtime, particularly when facing unexpected issues or complex control flows. Despite being a nearly universal practice, systematic study has been limited because these logs are ephemeral: they usually remain only in local environments and are removed before code is committed, making them difficult to capture. In this work, we addressed this challenge by mining accidental commits where developers unintentionally left ad-hoc logs and later deleted them, and by analyzing live-streamed programming sessions to observe their use in practice.
  Using these methods, we constructed a large dataset across three major programming languages (Java, JavaScript, and Python), enabling the large-scale investigation of logging practices.
  Our analysis reveals both common and language-specific patterns in where and how developers rely on ad-hoc logs. Across languages, ad-hoc logs tend to appear in program regions that are harder to reason about at runtime. We also identify distinctive language-level patterns, such as frequent use in asynchronous and callback functions in JavaScript and in thread-related classes in Java. In addition, functions containing ad-hoc logs generally have higher cyclomatic complexity than the overall function population. Production logs show a similar association, consistent with logging serving as a means of observing runtime behavior in structurally complex functions. Together, these findings provide empirical insight into developers' runtime comprehension practices and offer a valuable dataset for researchers and tool builders seeking to better support debugging and logging.

</details>


### [12] [IRONPROOF: COBOL-to-Python Transpilation with SMT-Based Equivalence Checking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11073)
*Dominik Blain*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: IRONPROOF 将 COBOL 解析为中间表示、生成 Python，并用 Z3 就共享输入编码二者的等价性，输出可机器检验的等价证明（UNSAT）或反例（SAT），但该证明仅覆盖"Python 与中间表示一致"，不保证与真实 COBOL 实现等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: COBOL 向现代语言翻译可能改变程序语义，LLM 翻译无法提供等价性保证，因此需要一个可机器检验的等价性验证途径。

Method: 流程为：将 COBOL 解析为中间表示，生成 Python，将二者编码为共享输入上的 Z3 公式，随后输出等价的 UNSAT 证明或 SAT 反例；并采用 PIC 限定的溢出检测，以及与 GnuCOBOL 3.2.0 运行时的对照实验。

Result: 在 2,345 个 COBOL 文件中有 782 个进入检查流程：606 个（77.5%）被证明等价，101 个部分验证，无一被反驳，75 个在流程内部失败并计入分母。独立编写程序上的成功率为 52.6%（291 中 153），而自写程序为 92.3%。153 个独立证明中 37 个仅覆盖"输入未被编码器建模"的单次执行，606 个证明中仅 14 个对影响输出且被证明的输入做了全称量化。在两个公开业务应用语料（AWS CardDemo、IBM GenApp）上，编码器无法端到端建模任何程序。2026 年 4 月的纯 LLM 基线在 94 个独立编写程序中失败 54 个。PIC 溢出检测在可分析程序中以 22.1% 的比例标记越界输出，属上界。与 GnuCOBOL 3.2.0 对照发现，49 个可执行独立证明中有 24 个在证明域内某变量上与运行时不一致。

Conclusion: 所生成的证明只确立"生成的 Python 与中间表示所宣称的 COBOL 语义一致"，并不等价于真实 COBOL 实现，且端到端覆盖率、量化强度与运行时一致性均存在明显局限。

Abstract: Translating COBOL to a modern language can change what a program computes, and LLM translations carry no guarantee of equivalence. We present IRONPROOF, which parses COBOL into an intermediate representation, generates Python, encodes both as Z3 formulas over shared inputs, and emits either a machine-checkable equivalence certificate (UNSAT) or a counterexample (SAT). On 2,345 COBOL files (GnuCOBOL tests, NIST CCVS85, open-source collections, and programs we generated or wrote), 782 enter the checking path: 606 (77.5%) are proved equivalent, 101 are partially verified, none is refuted, and 75 fail inside our pipeline and stay in the denominator. On independently authored programs the rate is 52.6% (153 of 291), against 92.3% on programs we wrote. Of the 153 independent proofs, 37 cover a single execution of a program that reads input the encoder does not model, and across all 606 proofs only 14 quantify over an input that a proved output depends on. On two public business-application corpora (AWS CardDemo and IBM GenApp), the encoder models no program end to end. An April 2026 LLM-only baseline failed on 54 of 94 independently authored programs, counting translations our encoder could not verify. PIC-bounded overflow detection flags an out-of-range output in 22.1% of the programs it can analyze, an upper bound. An earlier confrontation with GnuCOBOL 3.2.0 found 24 of 49 executable independent proofs disagreeing with the runtime on a variable in the proof's domain. A proof establishes that the generated Python computes what our intermediate representation says the COBOL computes, not equivalence to a COBOL implementation.

</details>


### [13] [Skill Constellations: Tracing the Supply Chain of Agent Skills on GitHub](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11169)
*Fahd Seddik*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文构建了首个基于 git 历史的 agent skill 复制网络（Skill Constellations），覆盖 GitHub 上 2,193,119 次 SKILL.md 采用，揭示 skill 作为无注册表、无版本、无溯源的供应链风险，并提出基于复制模型的审计排序方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 编码代理（如 Claude Code、Codex）执行的 SKILL.md 指令与脚本以用户权限运行，开发者通过在仓库间复制来共享 skill，形成缺乏注册表、版本与溯源的软件供应链。由此导致复制来源、安全修复的覆盖范围、需审查的仓库均不可知；仅记录单一时点持有情况的静态研究无法揭示"谁复制了谁"。

Method: 从 GitSkills 中每个 SKILL.md 的 git 历史构建加时间戳的复制网络，覆盖 GitHub 上 2,193,119 次 skill 采用，并提供交互式查看器；拟合"哪些仓库被他人复制"的模型，据此对仓库进行审计排序。

Result: 少数仓库是绝大多数复制的源头，且 GitHub star 数无法识别这些源头仓库；skill 副本几乎不随源更新，源头的修复很少传达到副本。按模型排序审查前 100 个仓库可阻止 14.9% 后续高风险 skill 采用，而审查 star 最多的 100 个仓库仅 0.5%。

Conclusion: 平台应分发带版本的引用（versioned references）而非副本；该复制网络与审计排序为安全工程师提供了在 skill 扩散前需检查的短名单。

Abstract: Agent skills are SKILL.md instructions and scripts that AI coding agents such as Claude Code and Codex run with the permissions of their user. Developers share skills by copying them between repositories, which makes them a software supply chain without a registry, versions or provenance. The origin of a copied skill, the reach of a security fix and the repositories that warrant review are therefore unknown. Studies that record which repositories hold a skill at a single point in time cannot reveal who copied it from whom. We contribute the first dated copy network of agent skills, built from the git history of every SKILL.md in GitSkills and covering 2,193,119 skill adoptions across GitHub, together with an interactive viewer. A few repositories are the source of almost all copies, and GitHub stars do not identify them. Skill copies almost never change with their source, and a fix at the source therefore rarely reaches them. We fit a model of which repositories others copy from and use it to rank repositories for audit. Reviewing the 100 repositories it ranks highest prevents 14.9% of later adoptions of high-risk skills, against 0.5% for the 100 most starred, which gives security engineers a short list to check before a skill spreads. Platforms should therefore distribute versioned references rather than copies. Project Website: https://fahdseddik.github.io/Skill-Constellations/

</details>


### [14] [Who Pays the Review Cost? Triage, Fairness, and Accountability in AI-authored Pull Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11179)
*Md Shamimur Rahman,Khairul Alam,Banani Roy,Chanchal K. Roy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过对31国239名代码评审者的混合方法问卷调查，论文发现AI作者身份并非PR被拒的绝对信号，评审者关注的是AIPR是否"可问责"——即有限范围、项目相关理由、CI之外的验证、贡献者响应性和可识别的合并后归属；作者提出"可评审性债务"（reviewability debt）概念来概括缺失这些要素时评审者被迫承担的工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码代理正从本地辅助走向基于pull request的协作工作流，AI生成的贡献必须嵌入既有项目规范中接受评审与维护。已有研究开始刻画AI作者PR（AIPRs），但对评审者如何把控其进入评审流程、AI作者身份如何重塑可信度与公平性，以及哪些准入机制能保护评审可持续性仍知之甚少。

Method: 采用混合方法问卷调查，覆盖31个国家的239名具备代码评审经验、对AIPR接触程度不同的从业者。通过情境（scenarios）与自我报告（self-reports）两类方式收集数据，并结合质性回应进行归纳分析。

Result: 受访者并未将AI作者身份视为一刀切的拒绝信号，而是将评审投入视为条件性的，取决于AIPR是否作为"可问责贡献"到达，具体包括：范围有界、理由扎根于项目、CI之外的验证、贡献者响应性、可识别的合并后归属。该条件逻辑同样适用于新人AIPR，受访者更看重其在当前评审过程中的可见参与，而非单纯的档案声誉。质性回应还揭示了当人类主导难以被观察时，指导、审查、延迟与路由等方面的转变。

Conclusion: 作者将缺失理由、验证与归属概括为"可评审性债务"（reviewability debt），即生成代码缺乏充分人类根基时评审者不得不吸收的额外工作。这一发现重构了AIPR治理方向：应在生成贡献消耗稀缺评审注意力之前，使人类判断变得可观察。

Abstract: AI coding agents are moving from local code assistance into pull-based workflows, where generated contributions must be reviewed, explained, and maintained within existing project norms. Although recent work has begun to characterize AI-authored pull requests (AIPRs), less is known about how reviewers govern their entry into review, how AI authorship reshapes credibility and fairness, and what intake mechanisms protect review sustainability. We report a mixed-method questionnaire survey of 239 practitioners from 31 countries with code-review experience and varying exposure to AIPRs. In the scenarios and self-reports elicited by the survey, AI authorship was not a categorical rejection signal. Instead, respondents described review effort as conditional on whether an AIPR arrived as an accountable contribution, with bounded scope, project-grounded rationale, validation beyond Continuous Integration (CI), contributor responsiveness, and identifiable post-merge ownership. This conditional logic extended to newcomer AIPRs, where respondents emphasized visible participation in the current review process over profile-level reputation alone. Qualitative responses further described shifts in mentoring, scrutiny, deferral, and routing when human stewardship was difficult to observe. We conceptualize missing rationale, validation, and ownership as reviewability debt, the work reviewers must absorb when generated code lacks sufficient human grounding. These findings reframe AIPR governance around making human judgment observable before generated contributions consume scarce reviewer attention.

</details>


### [15] [Characterizing Overconfident Failure in LLM-Based Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11300)
*Ravishka Rathnasuriya,Wei Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究代码大模型的"过度自信困境"：错误程序常以与正确程序相当的置信度被生成。作者在4个开源代码模型、3个执行型基准上分析不确定性指标能否作为执行正确性的代理，刻画全局与token级的过度自信，并评估常见缓解策略，最终得出四项发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM自动代码生成中，程序可能在语法上看似合理却无法通过执行正确性检查；现有验证方法（测试、程序分析）往往不完整、代价高或仅在生成后施加。模型自身的不确定性本应是早期的可靠性信号，因此需要考察其是否可信——尤其是错误程序被高置信度生成的问题。

Method: 实证研究：覆盖4个开源代码模型与3个基于执行的基准。首先检验现有不确定性指标是否为执行正确性的可靠代理；随后在全局与局部token层面刻画过度自信，考察置信度与熵摘要（含选择性生成、指令微调的局限）能否区分错误与正确程序；最后评估常见缓解策略；并进行探索性潜表示分析。

Result: 四项发现：(1) 现有不确定性信号对执行失败仅有部分且依赖模型的指示力；(2) 过度自信在程序级与token级均持续存在，基于不确定性的选择不能稳定提升接受集准确率；(3) 指令微调会提高失败生成的确定性，却未持续改善正确性判别；(4) 常见缓解技术改善可靠性的特定方面，但无法可靠解决过度自信型失败。潜分析提示隐藏表示可能编码了输出置信度未暴露的正确性信号。

Conclusion: 不确定性作为代码生成可靠性的早期信号并不可靠：错误与正确程序在置信度上难以区分，现有缓解手段有限。输出置信度可能掩盖了模型内部（隐藏表示）已编码的正确性相关信息，这为未来利用内部表征进行正确性判别提供了方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for automated code generation, but generated programs can appear syntactically plausible while still failing execution-based correctness checks. Existing validation methods, such as testing and program analysis, remain essential but are often incomplete, costly, or applied only after generation. Model-derived uncertainty is therefore a natural early reliability signal. This paper studies the dilemma of overconfidence in code LLMs where incorrect programs are often generated with token-level confidence comparable to correct programs. We study this dilemma across four open-source code models and three execution-based benchmarks. Our analysis begins by investigating whether existing uncertainty metrics provide reliable proxies for execution correctness in code generation. We then characterize overconfidence at both global and local token levels, asking whether incorrect programs remain indistinguishable from correct ones under confidence and entropy summaries, including selective generation and the limits of instruction tuning. Finally, we evaluate whether common mitigation strategies reduce this failure mode. Our study yields four findings. First, existing uncertainty signals provide only partial and model-dependent evidence of execution failure. Second, overconfidence persists at both program and token levels, and uncertainty-based selection does not consistently improve accepted-set accuracy. Third, instruction tuning can increase certainty on failing generations without consistently improving correctness discrimination. Fourth, common mitigation techniques improve specific aspects of reliability but do not reliably resolve overconfident failure. Our exploratory latent analysis suggests that hidden representations may encode correctness-related signals that output confidence does not expose.

</details>


### [16] [Closed-loop evaluation of LLM agents for embedded software development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11447)
*Jorge García-Carrasco,Sergio García-Carrasco,Alejandro Maté,Juan Trujillo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向嵌入式编码智能体的闭环评测基准，包含5个嵌入式控制任务与4种反馈场景，基于模拟ESP32固件，对7个GPT/Qwen配置进行420次运行评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM正被用作可迭代修复软件的编码智能体，而嵌入式固件正确性依赖传感、时序与安全约束下的闭环行为，现有评测多局限于一次性生成或离线正确性，缺乏闭环评估。

Method: 构建闭环评测基准：每个任务提供纯文本工程描述、受限工作区与可见的构建—运行接口；智能体需将需求转化为实现与自验证步骤并迭代。基准含5个嵌入式控制任务、4种反馈场景（一次性生成、真实自验证、CI式红/绿反馈、oracle式详细反馈），实现面向可复现的模拟ESP32固件。评测7个GPT系列与Qwen系列配置，每条件重复3次，共420次运行。

Result: gpt-5.4在受评配置中通过率最高但未使基准饱和；qwen3.5-27B为表现最强的本地模型；较小本地模型在通过率与搜索效率上急剧下降。

Conclusion: 结果表明白主可控的本地嵌入式编码智能体正在兴起，闭环评测基准为嵌入式智能体能力提供了更贴近真实工程流程的度量。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as coding agents that edit files, run builds and tests, inspect execution results, and repair software iteratively. Embedded firmware is a demanding target because correctness depends on closed-loop behavior under sensing, timing, and safety constraints, not only on static source quality. Yet embedded-agent evaluation remains limited and often emphasizes one-shot synthesis or offline correctness.
  We present a benchmark for closed-loop evaluation of embedded coding agents. Each task provides a plain-text engineering description, constrained workspace, and visible build-and-runtime surface. The agent must translate requirements into implementation and self-verification steps, then iterate until the required device behavior is achieved. The suite contains five embedded-control tasks and four feedback scenarios: one-shot generation, realistic self-verification, CI-style red/green feedback, and oracle-style detailed feedback.
  The implementation targets simulated ESP32 firmware for reproducibility. We evaluate seven GPT-family and Qwen-family configurations across five tasks and four scenarios, with three repetitions per condition for 420 runs. gpt-5.4 has the highest pass rate among evaluated configurations but does not saturate the benchmark; qwen3.5-27B is the strongest observed local model; and smaller local models degrade sharply in pass rate and search efficiency. These results suggest that capable local embedded coding agents are emerging.

</details>


### [17] [Evaluating Local Language Model Agents for Reproducible Data Engineering: An Empirical Software Engineering Study of Mobility Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11482)
*Jorge García-Carrasco,Javier Sanchis,Alejandro Reina-Reina,Alejandro Maté,Juan Trujillo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文构建了15项移动性数据工程任务的基准，用确定性检查器评估10种本地开源权重LLM智能体配置，发现在闭环工作区条件下、参数量大于20亿的模型通过率提升26.7–52.0个百分点，最强配置达85.3%，量化90亿参数模型达69.3%且内存约6.5 GB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体日益被用作软件与数据工程助手，但关于可本地部署的开源权重智能体的证据仍然有限；现有评测多侧重文本响应或孤立的代码生成，而非完整工程产物的有效性。因此作者希望评估本地LLM智能体能否产出正确且可复现的数据工程产物，并量化闭环工作区条件的影响及各维度权衡。

Method: 提出包含15项移动性工作流任务的基准，覆盖数据发现、连接器、交通流馈送处理、语义增强、特征工程、验证、可视化与报告；使用确定性检查器评估生成的脚本、表格、结构化文件、图表与报告；在消费级GPU上评估10种本地配置，分别在单次生成与闭环两种条件下，每个模型、模式与任务重复5次，共获得1500次评分尝试。

Result: 在参数量大于20亿的模型中，工作区（闭环）条件相比单次生成将通过率提升26.7–52.0个百分点；最强配置达到85.3%的产物级成功率；量化后的90亿参数模型在约6.5 GB内存占用下达到69.3%；当中间产物能暴露智能体可检查并修复的错误时，增益最大。

Conclusion: 本地开源权重智能体可支撑软件密集型数据工程工作中一个有意义的子集，但可靠性取决于模型能力、任务可验证性与确定性验证；该基准为在工程工作流中采用前评估完整智能体配置提供了可复现的方法。

Abstract: Context: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly used as software and data-engineering assistants, yet evidence about locally deployable open-weight agents remains limited. Existing evaluations often emphasize textual responses or isolated code generation rather than the validity of complete engineering artifacts.
  Objectives: We evaluate whether local LLM agents can produce correct and reproducible data-engineering artifacts, quantify the effect of a closed-loop workspace condition, and examine trade-offs in model scale, architecture, quantization, runtime, tool use, and failure.
  Methods: We introduce a benchmark of fifteen mobility-workflow tasks covering data discovery, connectors, transport-feed processing, semantic enrichment, feature engineering, validation, visualization, and reporting. Deterministic checkers assess generated scripts, tables, structured files, figures, and reports. Ten local configurations are evaluated in one-shot and closed-loop conditions, with five repetitions per model, mode, and task, yielding 1,500 scored attempts on a consumer-grade GPU.
  Results: Among models larger than two billion parameters, the workspace condition increases pass rates by 26.7-52.0 percentage points over one-shot generation. The strongest configuration reaches 85.3% artifact-level success, and a quantized 9-billion-parameter model reaches 69.3% with an approximately 6.5 GB memory footprint. Gains are largest when intermediate artifacts expose errors the agent can inspect and repair.
  Conclusion: Local open-weight agents can support a meaningful subset of software-intensive data-engineering work, but reliability depends on model capability, task verifiability, and deterministic validation. The benchmark provides a reproducible method for evaluating complete agent configurations before adoption in engineering workflows.

</details>


### [18] [SSCBench: Evaluating the Evidential Validity of Fault-Injection Tests for Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11514)
*Xincheng He,Wanli Dong,Zhaoqiang Guo,Yan Liu,Lei Xu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了LLM智能体故障注入评估中的证据有效性问题：智能体自身控制可见的权威观测，导致"采纳注入故障即失败"的推断未必成立。作者提出测量协议并构建SSCBench，量化每次执行实际实现的证据条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 故障注入被广泛用于评估使用工具的LLM智能体可靠性，但既有研究很少探讨：当智能体自身决定哪些权威观测在执行中可见时，故障采纳结果应如何解释。若关键反证可能在事实被首次使用之后才可见，则"采纳即失败"的结论在证据上不成立。

Method: 作者提出一套测量协议，明确三要素：哪些观测能反驳被注入的断言；这些观测能否在受影响事实被首次使用前变为可见；被评估的执行是否真正实现了该条件。据此构建SSCBench作为协议实例，在2个τ-bench环境中评估4种故障算子与5种智能体配置，共1,191次注入故障的执行，并进行轨迹分析。

Result: 实验表明：同一故障案例与智能体配置在不同执行中可实现的证据条件差异显著；即使支持"及时反证"主张的样本群稀疏或缺失，聚合采纳率仍可保持"良定义"。在44次最终出现反证的被采纳执行中，仅17次在首次使用前获得反证，27次在使用之后才获得。首次错误时机与后续立场修正并不必然一致；自动化轨迹分析可恢复采纳判定，但难以可靠恢复时序诊断所需的首次错误依赖事件。

Conclusion: 执行实际实现的证据条件、以及支撑特定主张的样本群，本身就是故障注入评估的组成部分；在将采纳解释为"使用前反证条件下的失败"之前，应先行报告这些信息。

Abstract: Fault injection is increasingly used to evaluate the reliability of tool-using LLM agents. However, there has been limited study of how fault-adoption results should be interpreted when the agent itself determines which authoritative observations become visible during execution. In this paper, we present a systematic study of this evidential validity problem in agent fault-injection evaluation. We develop a measurement protocol that specifies what observations can refute an injected assertion, determines whether they can become visible before the affected fact is first used, and records whether the evaluated execution actually realizes this condition. We construct SSCBench as an instantiation of the protocol and evaluate four fault operators and five agent configurations over 1,191 faulted executions in two $τ$-bench environments. Our experiments show that an admitted fault case and agent configuration can realize substantially different evidential conditions across executions, and that aggregate adoption can remain well defined even when the population supporting a timely-counterevidence claim is sparse or absent. For example, among 44 adopted runs in which counterevidence eventually became visible, only 17 received it before first use, while 27 received it afterward. We also find that first-error timing and later stance revision need not coincide, and that automated trajectory analysis can recover adoption without reliably recovering the first faulty-reliance event needed for temporal diagnosis. We argue that the evidential condition realized by an execution and the population supporting a claim-specific interpretation are part of fault-injection evaluation itself and should be reported before adoption is interpreted as failure under pre-use counterevidence.

</details>


### [19] [Chronos Enables Code Agents to Reason over Software Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11578)
*Xin Yin,Yiang Zhang,Zhiyuan Peng,Chao Ni,Zhe Cui,Xiaohua Xin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Chronos 是一个测试时框架，将历史 pull request 蒸馏为结构化经验卡片并构建带类型的关系图，通过语义检索与加权多跳扩展为 LLM 代码智能体提供仓库经验，在 SWE-Bench 系列基准上一致提升解决率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史 pull request 记录了代码库的设计决策、兼容性约束与实现模式，但相关经验往往分散在描述侧重各异的关联变更中，现有 LLM 代码智能体难以在测试时有效利用这种相互关联的历史经验。

Method: Chronos 将已合入的 PR 蒸馏为结构化经验卡片，并通过代码层、开发者意图与组织关系构成的带类型图进行连接；以语义搜索确定入口卡片，再用加权多跳扩展检索关联变更以供选择性阅读。同一记忆同时指导候选生成与补丁选择：补丁导向的变更智能体与验证策略智能体各自生成补丁，再由演化管家参考历史在二者间做出选择。

Result: 在 SWE-Bench Verified 上，完整流程在全部六个评测 LLM 骨干上均优于 SWE-Agent，平均解决率从 69.2% 提升至 72.9%，配合 MiniMax M2.5 达到 79.8%；同一骨干下，SWE-Bench Pro 从 48.3% 升至 51.7%，FEA-Bench Lite 从 41.0% 升至 43.5%。经验引导的单智能体变体也均优于基线。在 100 个任务、每任务检索十张卡片的人工评估中，图驱动检索将有用卡片均值从 1.24 提升至 2.87。

Conclusion: 结果表明，PR 之间的关系对于检索有用的仓库经验具有价值，且所评估的工作流能有效在补丁生成与选择过程中应用这些经验。

Abstract: Historical pull requests record the design decisions, compatibility constraints, and implementation patterns behind a codebase's current state. Experience relevant to a new task can span related changes whose descriptions emphasize different concerns. We introduce Chronos, a test-time framework that makes this connected history available to large language model (LLM)-based code agents. Chronos distills merged pull requests into structured experience cards and connects them through a typed graph of code-level, developer-intent, and organizational relations. Semantic search identifies entry cards, and weighted multi-hop expansion retrieves connected changes for selective reading. The same memory guides candidate generation and patch selection: a patch-focused change agent and a validation-strategy agent each develop a patch, and an evolution steward consults history to select between them. On SWE-Bench Verified, the full workflow improves SWE-Agent across all six evaluated LLM backbones, raising the mean resolution rate from 69.2% to 72.9% and reaching 79.8% with MiniMax M2.5. With the same backbone, it raises resolution rates from 48.3% to 51.7% on SWE-Bench Pro and from 41.0% to 43.5% on FEA-Bench Lite. Both experience-guided single-agent variants also outperform the base agent. In a human evaluation on 100 tasks with ten cards retrieved per task, graph-grounded retrieval increases the mean number of useful cards from 1.24 to 2.87 over flat semantic retrieval. These results demonstrate the value of PR relations for retrieving useful repository experience and of the evaluated workflows for applying that experience during patch generation and selection.

</details>


### [20] [Runnable Commit Untangling for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11593)
*Jinfeng Jiang,Dongsun Kim,Dayi Lin,Zhou Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文指出现有"提交解缠绕"研究的两大不足（未考虑解缠绕提交的有序性与可运行性、未直接验证维护收益），提出首个保持每次提交后代码可运行的 agentic 解缠绕方法 RucTangle 及首个量化解缠绕提交历史对编码智能体修复缺陷帮助的评估框架 TangleEval。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体产生的补丁庞大且纠缠，混合多种开发目的，难以审查与维护；提交解缠绕有望将其整理为可管理的提交。但现有研究存在两点局限：(1) 未考虑解缠绕后的提交是有序的，且应保证代码可运行，而实践中维护者不太可能接受导致代码无法运行的提交；(2) 现有研究仅通过解缠绕提交与开发者原始提交之间的语法比较来声称解缠绕有助于维护，却未直接展示所声称的维护收益。

Method: 提出两项新贡献：(1) RucTangle——首个在解缠绕提交的同时保证每次提交后代码均可运行的 agentic 方法；(2) TangleEval——首个量化"解缠绕、可管理的提交历史"如何帮助编码智能体修复缺陷的评估框架。实验上将 RucTangle 与四种解缠绕方法在 131 个智能体生成的补丁上进行比较；并收集 453 个引入回归（导致原本通过的测试失败）的智能体生成补丁，要求另外两个编码智能体修复回归，通过将 RucTangle 生成的历史加入智能体上下文来评估效果，同时分析智能体轨迹以了解其如何利用解缠绕提交来定位与修复缺陷。

Result: RucTangle 生成的所有历史均可运行，而基线方法产生 20.6%–37.4% 不可运行的提交历史。在回归修复任务中，将 RucTangle 生成的历史加入智能体上下文可带来 pass@1 的 5.2% 绝对提升。轨迹分析进一步揭示智能体如何利用解缠绕提交来导航与修复缺陷。

Conclusion: 研究表明在编码智能体时代采用成熟软件工程实践具有价值，并拓展了未来研究议程：智能体如何主动利用软件历史做出更好的开发决策。

Abstract: Coding agents produce large, tangled patches that mix multiple development purposes, making the code hard to review and maintain. Commit untangling offers the promise of organizing such large patches into untangled, manageable commits. This paper emphasizes two important limitations in existing commit untangling studies. First, they do not consider that untangled commits are ordered and should leave the code runnable. In practice, maintainers are unlikely to accept commits that prevent the code from running. Second, existing studies claim that commit untangling helps software maintenance. However, they conduct syntactic comparisons between the untangled commits and developers' original commits without directly showing the claimed maintenance benefits. To address these gaps, this paper makes two novel contributions: (1) RucTangle, the first agentic method that untangles commits while keeping the code runnable after each commit; and (2) TangleEval, the first evaluation framework that quantifies how untangled, manageable commit histories help coding agents repair bugs. We compare RucTangle against four untangling methods on 131 agent-generated patches. All histories produced by RucTangle are runnable, while baselines produce 20.6%-37.4% unrunnable commit histories. We further collect 453 agent-generated patches that introduce regressions (i.e., causing previously passing tests to fail) and ask two other coding agents to repair regressions. Augmenting agent context with RucTangle-produced histories yields 5.2% absolute improvement in pass@1. We also analyze agent trajectories to learn how they use untangled commits to navigate and fix bugs. Our findings demonstrate the value of adopting established software engineering practices in the era of coding agents, which broaden the future research agenda: how can agents actively use software history to make better development decisions?

</details>


### [21] [PolyCodeEval: Benchmarking Multilingual Code Generation from Functions to Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11618)
*Bowen Yang,Jiajun Jiang,Luxue Yu,Yihao Wang,Fengjie Li,Dong Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出统一的多语言、多粒度代码生成基准PolyCodeEval（2590个任务、5种语言、58个真实仓库），采用统一执行协议评测主流模型、专用方法与编码智能体，揭示现有方法在函数/文件/仓库级生成上仍存在显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型走向仓库级软件工程，现有代码生成基准在语言覆盖、任务粒度和评测协议上高度碎片化，难以进行系统性对比，因此需要构建统一基准。

Method: 构建PolyCodeEval：包含2590个从函数到仓库级的代码生成任务，源自5种编程语言的58个可执行开源仓库；所有任务在统一的基于执行的协议下评测，并针对不同生成目标定制集成流程；在此基础上评测前沿大模型、最先进专用方法和通用编码智能体，并开展成对实验分析上下文影响。

Result: 现有方法在各粒度与语言上仍难以正确生成完整代码片段：函数、文件、仓库级最高正确率分别仅为71.7%、76.7%、31.0%，且跨语言性能差异显著；配对实验表明，同一文件中相关函数的实现上下文可提升函数生成的可执行正确性；方法排名随任务粒度与编程语言而变化。

Conclusion: 多语言、多粒度评测对全面评估代码生成能力至关重要，单一粒度或单一语言的评测不足以反映模型真实能力。

Abstract: As large language models increasingly move toward repository-level software engineering, existing code-generation benchmarks remain fragmented across language coverage, task granularity, and evaluation protocols, impeding systematic comparison. To address this gap, we present PolyCodeEval, a unified multilingual and multi-granularity benchmark for code generation. It comprises 2,590 code generation tasks spanning functions to repositories, derived from 58 real, executable open-source repositories in five programming languages. All tasks are evaluated under a unified execution-based protocol with integration procedures tailored to their generation targets. Building on this benchmark, we evaluate frontier large language models, state-of-the-art specialized methods, and general coding agents. Our results show that existing approaches still struggle to correctly generate complete code fragments across granularities and languages. Specifically, the studied methods generate at most 71.7%, 76.7%, and 31.0% correct functions, files, and repositories, respectively, with performance varying widely across languages. Paired experiments further show that implementation context from related functions in the same file improves the executable correctness of function generation. Method rankings also vary across task granularities and programming languages, highlighting the importance of multilingual, multi-granularity evaluation for comprehensively assessing code generation capabilities.

</details>


### [22] [One Skill Too Many: How Co-Installed Skills Conflict in Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11647)
*Chaoliang Yan,Zihao Xu,Yuekang Li,Shangzhi Xu,Yi Liu,Gelei Deng,Siqi Ma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次实证研究了编码代理中"相似技能冲突"问题：当两个功能相同的技能被共同安装时，模型仅凭名称和描述选择，导致已安装技能的核心功能被替代却仍能通过任务完成度基准测试；作者通过大规模挖掘与实验提出应以"独占核心功能"作为评测指标，并在首次技能读取处设置钩子以恢复保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码代理通过 SKILL.md 目录扩展"代理技能"，但技能来源多样（团队、开发者、插件、复制集合），导致功能相同的相似技能可能被共同安装，模型仅凭名称和描述进行选择。在冲突情形下，已安装技能会丢失核心功能（如禁止操作 git），但因任务仍然完成，仅检查任务完成度的基准测试无法发现此类问题。作者旨在首次系统性地实证研究这一冲突现象。

Method: 从 20,947 个仓库快照中挖掘出 822,109 个候选相似技能对；由 LLM 评判其中 3,754 个分层样本；对 312 个确认的相似对在三个模型上运行实验（6,368 次运行、169,294 次工具调用、542 个代理小时），并分析安装位置、列举顺序、首次技能读取时机及预工具钩子的影响。

Result: 五项发现：(1) 冲突易发技能普遍，近四分之一已安装技能与功能相同的技能共存，37% 被判技能位于复制集合中；(2) 此类配对多为规范性技能，其次为能力技能；(3) 在不降低任务完成度的情况下，相似技能在五分之一运行中取代已安装技能，先打开相似技能的运行会丢失超过三分之一的独占核心功能；(4) 安装位置决定哪个技能运行，列举顺序几乎无关，仅 0.9% 的替代运行在最终回复中说明所用技能；(5) 冲突在首次技能读取时即被决定，几乎总在文件更改前，在读取处设置预工具钩子可将独占核心功能保真度恢复到先打开已安装技能的水平。

Conclusion: 基准测试应评分"独占核心功能"而非仅任务完成度；平台应守护首次技能读取并显示实际运行的技能，以避免相似技能冲突导致的隐性功能丧失。

Abstract: Coding agents are extended with agent skills, directories whose SKILL.md tells the model when and how to perform a task. Because skills come from independent sources (teams, developers, plugins, copied collections), an installed skill can be co-installed with a similar skill doing the same job, and the model picks between them by name and description alone. In a conflict, the installed skill loses core functions (e.g., a ban on touching git) because the similar skill runs instead or changes what it does. The task still passes, so benchmarks that check only task completion miss such cases. We present the first empirical study of such conflicts. From snapshots of 20,947 repositories, we mine 822,109 candidate similar-skill pairs, have an LLM judge a stratified sample of 3,754, and run 312 confirmed pairs on three models (6,368 runs, 169,294 tool calls, 542 agent-hours). We report five findings. (1) Conflict-prone skills are common: nearly one in four installed skills is co-installed with one that does the same job, and 37% of judged skills sit inside copied collections. (2) Most such pairs involve normative skills, then capability skills. (3) Without lowering task completion, a similar skill takes one in five runs from the installed skill, and runs that open the similar skill first lose over a third of the exclusive core functions that only the installed skill fulfills. (4) Install location decides which skill runs, listing order barely matters, and the final reply names the skill used in only 0.9% of substituted runs. (5) Conflicts are decided at the first skill read, almost always before any file is changed, and a pre-tool hook at that read restores fidelity on exclusive core functions to the level of runs that open the installed skill first. Benchmarks should thus score exclusive core functions, and platforms should guard the first read and show which skill ran.

</details>


### [23] [Implementing the Spec Growth Engine: Preventing Spec-Code Divergence, and Growing the Spec with Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11725)
*Hartwig Grabowski*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍 Spec Growth Engine 的实现：以规范图为核心，将"规范-代码一致性校验"与"智能体驱动的规范生长"拆为两层，确定性层负责校验、证据评级与变更影响分类，智能体层通过意图作者、规划者、编码者三角色分轮扩展图，并以确定性规则决定是否续跑。三层独立开关（草案闸门、破坏性变更委托、运行模式）叠加两种"项目基线"设置，构成 18 种运行方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 辅助软件开发中，代码与规范易发生偏离，且智能体自主性缺乏可度量的确定性依据。作者希望在不依赖模型调用的前提下锚定开发过程，并让"人类判断的委托程度"成为可显式配置的变量。

Method: 双层架构。第一层为确定性引擎：校验规范图、与代码导入图比对、依据记录的测试证据授予节点"已验证"状态、按可破坏范围对每次变更分类，全程不调用模型。第二层为智能体规范生长：意图作者、规划者、编码者各由独立模型扮演，分轮扩展图，每轮后由确定性规则判定是否继续；通过闸门、请求与豁免实现人机通信。

Result: 设计了三种独立开关（草案闸门、破坏性变更委托、运行模式）与两种项目基线铺设方式，组合出 18 种项目运行方式；覆盖从全手动到无监督运行（人类事后审阅决策）的完整操作谱系，并明确了各方式的闸门、请求与豁免机制。

Conclusion: 贯穿设计的一贯主张是：自主运行的价值上限，取决于度量它的那个确定性实例。

Abstract: The Spec Growth Engine anchors AI-assisted software development in a graph of specifications that the code is coupled to. This paper describes its implementation, which serves two tasks and keeps them apart as two layers. The first layer prevents spec-code divergence: a deterministic engine validates the spec graph, compares it with the code's import graph, earns a node's verified status from recorded test evidence, and classifies every change by what it can break -- without calling a model. The second layer grows the spec with agents: an intent author, a planner and a coder, each played by its own model, extend the graph in rounds, and a deterministic rule decides after each round whether the run goes on. How much of the human's judgement is delegated is set by three independent switches -- a draft gate, a delegation for breaking changes, and the run mode -- which, with two ways of laying a project's floor, give eighteen ways to run a project. We describe each of them, the gates, requests and waivers through which agents and the human communicate, and the spectrum of operation from entirely manual work to an unsupervised run whose decisions the human reviews afterwards. Throughout, one claim holds the design together: an autonomous run is worth only as much as the deterministic instance that measures it.

</details>


### [24] [MAP4CS: A Multi-dimensional Data Pruning Framework for Efficient Code Retriever Fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11727)
*Yuxuan Chen,Mingwei Liu,Guangsheng Ou,Zekai Zhang,Zike Li,Yanlin Wang,Pelin Zheng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: MAP4CS：面向代码检索的多维感知剪枝框架，用仅5%的高质量核心子集超越随机采样，并达到甚至超过全量微调的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG已成为增强LLM领域知识的重要手段，但将检索器适配到不断演化的代码仓库仍具挑战：大规模代码语料存在噪声与冗余。全量微调计算昂贵，且因低质量样本的负迁移导致次优性能；而简单随机采样无法保证数据代表性。

Method: 提出MAP4CS（Multi-dimensional Awareness Pruning for Code Search）自适应数据剪枝框架：综合语法结构、语义多样性与分布表示，识别小而高质量的核心子集，并辅以严格的规则过滤流水线。

Result: 在两个大规模数据集上，MAP4CS仅使用5%训练数据即持续优于随机采样基线，性能与全量微调相当甚至更优，验证了以数据为中心AI中的“少即是多”假设。语言分析进一步揭示自适应优化机制。

Conclusion: MAP4CS可自动充当冗余语料的去重器与混乱语料的去噪器，构建词法多样且信息密集的训练语料，为代码检索中的检索器适配提供了高效的数据中心化方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has become a cornerstone in software engineering for enhancing Large Language Models (LLMs) with domain-specific knowledge. However, adapting retrievers to evolving code repositories remains challenging due to the noise and redundancy inherent in massive code corpora. Standard fine-tuning on the full corpus is computationally expensive and often leads to sub-optimal performance due to negative transfer from low-quality samples. Conversely, simple random sampling fails to guarantee data representativeness.
  To address these challenges, we propose MAP4CS (Multi-dimensional Awareness Pruning for Code Search), an adaptive data pruning framework. MAP4CS identifies a small, high-quality core subset by integrating syntactic structure, semantic diversity, and distributional representation, followed by a rigorous rule-based filtering pipeline. Extensive experiments on two large-scale datasets demonstrate that MAP4CS consistently outperforms random sampling baselines using only 5% of the training data. Remarkably, it achieves performance comparable to, or even superior to, fine-tuning on the full dataset, validating the ''less is more'' hypothesis in data-centric AI. Furthermore, linguistic analysis reveals an adaptive optimization mechanism: MAP4CS automatically functions as a de-duplicator for redundant corpora and a denoiser for chaotic ones, constructing a training corpus that is both lexically diverse and information-dense.

</details>


### [25] [ObliVul: Alert-Conditioned Safety Obligation Modeling and Bidirectional Counterfactual Validation for Code Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11801)
*Heyang Tan,Chengxin Gao,Xin Wen,Jiaxin Li,Rui Cao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出ObliVul框架，通过“告警条件化的安全义务建模”与“双向反事实验证”来提升漏洞检测精度，核心是先从CPG抽取局部证据包并由大模型恢复安全义务，再对齐程序节点、依赖边与路径范围构建局部安全义务图，最后聚合验证后的告警证据进行函数级漏洞预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实软件开发中，静态分析会产生大量候选告警，真正的挑战不是发现可疑代码，而是判断哪些告警值得关注。现有基于学习的方法多在函数级或行级识别可疑模式，难以围绕单个告警抽取完整程序证据；大模型虽能从局部程序事实推断风险源、危险操作、保护条件与状态前置条件，但这些语义信息难以与具体程序节点、依赖关系和传播路径可靠对齐，因而无法充分验证相应安全义务是否真正影响当前告警。

Method: ObliVul为告警条件化的安全义务建模与双向反事实验证框架：对每个候选告警，先从代码属性图（CPG）中抽取局部证据包（LEP），并利用大语言模型恢复候选安全义务；随后将安全义务与程序节点、依赖边及路径范围对齐，构建局部安全义务图（LSOG）；最后通过VAFI聚合互补的已验证告警证据，并抑制冗余或较弱证据，生成函数级漏洞预测。

Result: 实验结果表明，ObliVul能够有效区分漏洞版本与修复版本，并减少修复代码上持续存在的误报。消融研究进一步证实了各组件在安全义务恢复、风险响应验证以及函数级漏洞推断中的必要性。

Conclusion: ObliVul通过将大模型推断的安全义务与程序图结构进行对齐和双向反事实验证，缓解了静态分析告警筛选与函数级漏洞判定中的误报问题；消融结果说明安全义务恢复、风险响应验证与函数级漏洞推断三个环节缺一不可。

Abstract: In real-world software development, the primary challenge in vulnerability detection is often not finding suspicious code, but identifying which alerts among the large number of candidate alerts produced by static analysis truly warrant attention. Existing learning-based methods mainly identify suspicious patterns at the function or line level, making it difficult to extract complete program evidence centered on an individual alert. Although large language models can infer risk sources, dangerous operations, protection conditions, and state preconditions from local program facts, such semantic information cannot be reliably aligned with specific program nodes, dependency relations, and propagation paths, and is therefore insufficient to verify whether the corresponding safety obligations truly affect the current alert. To address this problem, we propose ObliVul, an alert-conditioned safety obligation modeling and bidirectional counterfactual validation framework for vulnerability detection. For each candidate alert, ObliVul first extracts a Local Evidence Pack (LEP) from the Code Property Graph (CPG) and uses a large language model to recover candidate safety obligations. It then aligns the safety obligations with program nodes, dependency edges, and path scopes to construct a Local Safety Obligation Graph (LSOG). Finally, VAFI aggregates complementary verified alert evidence while suppressing redundant or weaker evidence to produce a function-level vulnerability prediction. Experimental results show that ObliVul effectively distinguishes vulnerable versions from fixed versions and reduces persistent false positives on fixed code. Ablation studies further confirm the necessity of each component for safety obligation recovery, risk response validation, and function-level vulnerability inference.

</details>


### [26] [On the Risks of using LLM-Generated Tests for Regression Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11835)
*Mohammadali Charoosaei,Cedric Richter,Mike Papadakis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究揭示LLM生成的回归测试可能固化错误行为，形成新的技术债：在145个PR中8%-17%为"强制错误测试"，仅2.4%-4.8%为"揭示错误测试"，且前者长期存续、持续累积并大量逃逸开发者测试套件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM自动化生成回归测试虽能在实现正确时节省成本，但当被测代码本身含缺陷时，生成的测试可能将错误行为编码为"期望行为"并加以保留，这一潜在风险此前缺乏实证研究。

Method: 作者将LLM回归测试生成应用于SciPy、Qiskit、pandas三个项目主分支已合并的PR，追踪生成测试对后续项目演进的影响，并区分"揭示错误测试"（断言正确行为）与"强制错误测试"（断言错误行为）两类。

Result: 在145个PR中，8%-17%的生成测试为强制错误测试，仅2.4%-4.8%揭示错误；若干后续提交后83%-91%的强制错误测试仍相关且通过；将所有PR的缺陷合并到同一代码库后，83%-92%仍被强制保留，而开发者测试套件仅能检出14%-30%。

Conclusion: 缺乏人工验证时，LLM生成的回归测试会将错误行为编码为期望行为，使缺陷跨版本持续存在并规避开发者维护的测试套件，构成一种新型技术债。

Abstract: Software is under constant evolution: developers continuously add features, fix bugs, and refactor code, and any of these changes may break existing functionality. Regression testing guards against such effects by capturing expected behavior in test cases. LLM-based test generation aims to automate this process by generating regression tests directly from the code under test. This is beneficial when the implementation is correct, but problematic when the code contains faults: the generated tests may then encode and preserve incorrect behavior. To investigate this risk, we apply LLM-based regression test generation to pull requests merged into the main branch of software projects and study the impact of the generated tests on subsequent project evolution. We distinguish between fault-revealing tests, which assert correctly implemented behavior, and fault-enforcing tests, which assert faulty behavior. Across 145 pull requests from SciPy, Qiskit, and pandas, 8%-17% of the generated tests are fault-enforcing, while only 2.4%-4.8% reveal faults. Fault-enforcing tests persist over time: after several subsequent commits, 83%-91% of them are still relevant and pass. They also accumulate: when the faults of all pull requests are combined in one codebase, 83%-92% remain enforced at the end of the commit history, and the developer-written test suite detects only 14%-30% of them. Our results reveal a fundamental risk of LLM-generated regression tests: without manual validation, they may encode faulty behavior as expected behavior, allowing bugs to persist across software revisions and largely evade developer-maintained test suites. LLM-based regression testing can thus give rise to a new form of technical debt.

</details>


### [27] [Trajectory-Guided Fault Localization for Agent Skill Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11858)
*Yu Ge,Linna Xie,Zhong Li,Yu Pei,Tian Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SkillMorph：基于轨迹引导故障定位的智能体技能演化方法，将执行证据关联到技能内容后再生成修订，在两个基准上超越原始技能及四种现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 技能为代码智能体提供可复用指导，但不完整或不合适的指导会损害任务执行。现有用LLM从执行反馈生成修订的方法，难以将修订建立在明确的行为证据之上，即缺乏证据与技能内容的对应关系。

Method: 核心思想是在生成修订前先将执行证据链接到具体技能内容。方法对比重复运行与跨任务中抽象轨迹的成功/失败证据，并结合演化循环之间的变化识别可疑动作，进而用这些可疑动作定位技能中的编辑位置并生成相应修订。

Result: 在SWE-Skills-Bench和CannBot上，SkillMorph演化的技能在试验级准确率和执行一致性方面持续优于原始技能及四种现有技能演化方法。在AI运维开发团队的自动化内核生成场景中，已有6个技能修订PR被接受。

Conclusion: 轨迹引导的故障定位能有效将行为证据落地到技能内容，实现更可靠的技能演化，并具备实际工程落地价值。

Abstract: Agent skills provide reusable guidance for code agents, but incomplete or unsuitable guidance can impair task execution. To reduce the manual effort of skill refinement, recent approaches use LLMs to generate revisions from execution feedback. However, grounding these revisions in explicit behavioral evidence remains challenging. To address this gap, we propose SkillMorph, a skill-evolution approach based on trajectory-guided fault localization in agent skills. Its core idea is to link execution evidence to specific skill contents before generating revisions. Specifically, SkillMorph compares failure and success evidence in abstracted trajectories across repeated runs and tasks, incorporating changes between evolution loops to identify suspicious actions. It then uses these suspicious actions to localize edit sites in the skills and generate corresponding revisions. Experiments on SWE-Skills-Bench and CannBot show that the skills evolved by SkillMorph consistently achieve higher trial-level accuracy and execution consistency than the original skills and those from four existing skill-evolution methods. We have also applied SkillMorph to automated kernel generation with an AI operator-development team, which has accepted 6 skill-revision pull requests.

</details>


### [28] [Evaluating Exact Output and Checkpoint-State Prediction in Real Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11889)
*Xiaohong Chen,David Bucur,Chenglong Ma,Yi Zhang,Lingming Zhang,Sriram Vishwanath,Grigore Rosu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个基准，用于仅凭源代码与输入预测程序的最终输出及循环内/后的检查点状态，扩展了CRUXEval式输出预测任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有输出预测评测多聚焦短输出，短输出得分可能掩盖模型在长轨迹、循环内部状态跟踪等方面的错误，缺少能同时考察最终输出与中间检查点状态的基准。

Method: 构建含400个案例（来自371个Python与C++程序）的基准，配对短/长轨迹输入，并设置循环内与循环后检查点；在四个模型家族的七种设置下评测，不使用工具或代码执行，计划11,200次预测。

Result: 11,151条预测可评分；启用推理的设置比关闭推理高33.1–55.2个百分点。最优设置在短轨迹最终输出93.0%、长轨迹77.0%、两项状态任务65.5%与63.5%，缺失响应计为错误时仍成立。2,397组匹配Python对比中，改为长轨迹输入产生528次正确→错误变化与147次反转；源聚类分析保留该准确率差距，调整后Python模型未发现累计状态负载与错误存在正向增量关联。

Conclusion: 该基准揭示了仅靠短输出分数所掩盖的错误；但由于输入变化与检查点任务同时改变多个因素，性能差距不能单独归因于轨迹长度或内部状态跟踪机制。

Abstract: We present a benchmark for predicting final output and checkpoint state from source and input alone. It extends CRUXEval-style output prediction with paired shorter- and longer-trace inputs and checkpoints inside and after a loop. The benchmark contains 400 cases from 371 Python and C++ programs, evaluated under seven settings from four model families without tools or code execution. Of 11,200 planned predictions, 11,151 produced gradable responses. Reasoning-enabled settings outperform their off counterparts by 33.1 to 55.2 percentage points on completed responses. The strongest setting scores 93.0% on shorter-trace final output, 77.0% on longer-trace final output, and 65.5% and 63.5% on the two state tasks; these scores also hold when missing responses count as wrong. Across 2,397 matched Python comparisons with identical source, changing to the longer-trace input yields 528 correct-to-wrong changes and 147 reversals. Source-clustered analyses preserve this accuracy gap, while adjusted Python models give no evidence of a positive incremental association between cumulative state load and error. Changed inputs and checkpoint tasks alter several factors together, so the gaps do not isolate trace length or an internal state-tracking mechanism. The benchmark exposes errors hidden by short-output scores alone.

</details>


### [29] [Can LLMs Fix It Without Code? Toward Automated Verification of No-Code Bug Fixes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11963)
*Utku Boran Torun,Veli Karakaya,Eray Tüzün*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于执行（execution-based）的自动化流水线，在真实浏览器环境中评估LLM生成"无代码修复（no-code fix）"的能力。对322个由12种配置生成的无代码修复进行验证，结果显示仅14.6%~49.7%能真正解决所报告的bug，且结果高度依赖执行器（executor）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无效的bug报告可通过"无代码修复"（引导用户更改设置、升级版本或调整工作流）来解决，但人工验证这些修复是否真的解决问题耗时巨大，因此需要自动化验证手段，并系统评估LLM生成此类修复的实际能力。

Method: 构建基于执行的自动化评估流水线：执行器智能体（executor agent）按修复的自然语言指令执行操作，问题专用检查器（issue-specific checker）判断bug是否仍存在。评估322个无代码修复（覆盖Faulty Configuration、Wrong Version、External System & Dependency三类bug），使用三种执行器（两个计算机使用智能体OpenCUA-72B与Claude Sonnet 5，以及多模态智能体LLM Meta Muse Glimmer），并设置sanity gate过滤，最后与人工执行结果对比。

Result: 仅17.6%的候选问题能成功搭建并通过双重sanity gate；322个修复中，14.6%~49.7%成功解决bug（随执行器而异）。最强配置（Vanilla流水线中的Claude Opus 4.6）在Claude Sonnet 5下最高解决74.1%的修复。仅更换执行器会使某配置的解决率平均变化38.8%，三种执行器仅在46.9%的修复上给出一致判定；与人工共识对比，匹配率为66.1%~88.1%。

Conclusion: 即使在最优执行器下，不到半数的LLM生成无代码修复能真正解决所报告的bug，因此此类修复在到达用户前必须经过验证；基于执行的验证可提供这一保障，但测得的能力强烈依赖执行器，评估报告必须对其进行报告与控制。

Abstract: A no-code fix resolves an invalid bug report by directing the user to change a setting, update to a version where the problem is already fixed, or adjust their workflow. Manually verifying whether a proposed no-code fix resolves the reported bug takes considerable developer time. This study proposes an automated, execution-based pipeline for evaluating the capability of large language models (LLMs) to generate no-code fixes in a real browser environment. We evaluate 322 no-code fixes generated by the 12 configurations released with the benchmark of a previous study, covering bug reports categorized as Faulty Configuration, Wrong Version, or External System & Dependency. An executor agent applies each fix by following its natural-language instructions, and an issue-specific checker determines whether the reported bug persists. We repeat the pipeline with three executors: two Computer-Use Agents, OpenCUA-72B and Claude Sonnet 5, and one multimodal agentic LLM, Meta's Muse Glimmer. Only 17.6% of the candidate issues could be set up and passed both sanity gates. Across the 322 fixes, 14.6% to 49.7% resolved the bug depending on the executor, and the strongest configuration, Claude Opus 4.6 in the Vanilla pipeline, resolved up to 74.1% of its fixes under Claude Sonnet 5. Changing only the executor shifted a configuration's resolution rate by 38.8% on average, and the three executors reached the same verdict on only 46.9% of the fixes. Compared with human execution, the executors matched the human consensus for 66.1% to 88.1% of the sampled fixes. Even under the best executor, fewer than half of the LLM-generated no-code fixes resolve the reported bug, so such fixes need verification before they reach users. Execution-based verification can provide this, but the measured capability depends strongly on the executor, which evaluations must report and control.

</details>


### [30] [Neural Network Verification for Deep Joint Source-Channel Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11994)
*Thanh Le,Hai Duong,Takeshi Matsumura,ThanhVu Nguyen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出首个针对DeepJSCC解码器的界传播验证框架，通过扩展PReLU线性松弛、改写转置卷积、并将瑞利衰落建模为前置结构扰动，结合GloRo全局鲁棒训练，实现了对DeepJSCC模型最坏情况重构误差的紧致认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DeepJSCC端到端传输在对抗扰动与信道干扰下重构质量会急剧下降，但尚无方法能形式化地界定这种退化程度。同时现有DNN验证器无法支持DeepJSCC解码器中的PReLU、转置卷积和瑞利衰落三个组件。

Method: 1) 扩展DNN验证中线性松弛优化的SOTA技术以支持PReLU；2) 将转置卷积替换为其受限的"先上采样后卷积"形式；3) 将瑞利衰落建模为直接前置到解码器的结构扰动，从而降低验证问题维度；4) 实例化Lipschitz正则化的全局鲁棒训练GloRo。

Result: 在图像传输DeepJSCC模型上，结合结构编码的全局鲁棒训练使中位认证界降低最多41%，在信道估计10度误差下认证安全案例数约为GloRo加区间编码的十倍（192对19）。基于OFDM的软件无线电空口验证证实证书在真实硬件上成立，链路实测最坏误差0.082，低于认证界0.128。

Conclusion: 该框架首次实现对DeepJSCC模型的紧致认证，显著提升全局鲁棒性并大幅增加认证安全案例数，且经真实硬件空口验证证书有效，为DeepJSCC的形式化验证提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Deep joint source-channel coding (DeepJSCC) transmits data end-to-end over wireless channels using a neural encoder-decoder, but reconstruction quality can degrade sharply under adversarial perturbations and channel disturbances; no method formally bounds this degradation for DeepJSCC. We present the first bound-propagation framework for verifying DeepJSCC's decoder, bounding worst-case reconstruction error over a given wireless channel's noise region. Current deep neural network (DNN) verifiers do not support three DeepJSCC decoder components: parametric rectified linear activations (PReLU), transposed convolutions, and Rayleigh fading. We extend state-of-the-art techniques for optimization of linear relaxation in DNN verification for PReLU, replace the transposed convolution with its restricted upsample-then-convolution form, and formulate Rayleigh fading as a structural perturbation prepended directly into the decoder, thereby reducing the dimensionality of the verification problem. We also instantiate Lipschitz-regularized global robustness training, denoted GloRo, improving global robustness and enabling tight certification of DeepJSCC models for the first time. On DeepJSCC model for image transmission, this global robustness training procedure combined with structural encoding lowers the median certified bound by up to 41% and certifies about ten times more safe cases (192 against 19) than GloRo with interval encoding at a 10-degree error in channel estimation. Over-the-air validation with an orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing (OFDM) implementation on software-defined radio devices confirm the certificate holds on real hardware, with a worst observed error on radio link at 0.082 against a certified bound of 0.128.

</details>


### [31] [Reliability Characterization for N-version Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12149)
*Shunsuke Nagao,Fumio Machida*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对N版本目标检测缺乏可靠性与多样性度量的问题，提出Cov_OD和Cer_OD两个可靠性指标，并在自动驾驶仿真器案例中验证其可用于版本组合选择、投票策略选择与增量构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: N版本目标检测通过多模型或多输入帧多样化检测结果并聚合以减少错误，其可靠性依赖于多个检测结果间的多样性与一致性。但现有指标（如mAP、Accuracy）仅基于聚合后的最终结果定义，无法刻画这些特性，因此需要新的可靠性度量。

Method: 提出两个面向N版本目标检测的可靠性指标：目标检测误差覆盖度（Cov_OD）与目标检测准确预测确定性（Cer_OD）。二者可直接由各单独检测结果计算，不依赖投票策略。并通过自动驾驶仿真器中车辆检测的N版本目标检测案例研究，实证展示指标的独特性质。

Result: 所提指标可用于：1）选择具有互补误差特性的版本组合；2）基于多样性与一致性画像选择有效投票策略；3）通过成对两版本分析指导N版本目标检测系统的增量构建。

Conclusion: 结果表明，能够指导设计可靠N版本目标检测应用的度量指标具有重要意义。

Abstract: N-version object detection (OD) is an approach to diversifying detection results using multiple models or input frames and reducing detection errors by aggregating individual results. Diversity and consistency across multiple detection results are critical information for characterizing the reliability of possible configurations of N-version OD systems. However, existing performance metrics such as mAP and Accuracy fail to capture these factors, as they are defined solely on the final outcome after aggregation. To overcome this limitation, we propose two reliability metrics particularly defined for N-version OD, namely coverage of errors in OD (Cov_OD) and certainty of accurate prediction in OD (Cer_OD), which can be computed from individual detection results without relying on voting strategies. We empirically demonstrate the unique features of the proposed metrics through a case study of N-version OD for a vehicle in an autonomous-driving simulator. We show that the proposed metrics can be used for 1) selecting version combinations with complementary error characteristics, 2) choosing an effective voting strategy based on diversity and consistency profiles, and 3) guiding incremental construction of N-version OD systems via pairwise two-version analysis. These results highlight the importance of the metrics that can guide the design of reliable N-version OD applications.

</details>


### [32] [Cadence: Strategic Guidance for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12269)
*Minxing Wang,He Ye,Earl T. Barr,Yintong Huo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出动态监控框架Cadence，通过双层干预与自适应调度提升LLM编码代理可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM编码代理运行时监控器受限于静态触发机制：固定检查间隔会遗漏严重错误时的及时指导，同时在健康执行时产生额外开销；刚性启发式规则难以检测复杂推理错误。

Method: Cadence包含两个核心模块：双层干预模块（对正常执行和轻微失误提供建议级指导，对严重错误提供替换级指导）与检查调度器（依据干预级别调整检查频率，替换级后加紧监督，建议级后放松监督）。

Result: 在300个SWE-bench Lite任务、mini-swe-agent与Moatless两个代理上评估，Cadence在所用监控器中解析率最高。相比vanilla代理，mini-swe-agent提升25.33%（+76个解决任务），Moatless提升15.67%（+47个解决任务），同时与最先进基线保持有竞争力的token效率。

Conclusion: 基于执行健康度的动态监控与干预调度可有效提升LLM编码代理的任务解决率，并兼顾效率。

Abstract: Runtime monitors are increasingly used to improve the reliability of LLM-based coding agents by inspecting execution trajectories and delivering corrective guidance upon detecting misbehavior. However, their effectiveness remains limited by static guidance triggering schemes. Existing monitors rely either on fixed inspection intervals, missing timely guidance during severe misbehaviors while incurring unnecessary overhead during healthy execution, or on rigid heuristic rules, failing to detect complex reasoning errors. To address these limitations, we propose Cadence, a dynamic monitoring framework that adaptively schedules inspections and delivers guidance according to the agent's real-time execution health. Cadence consists of two core modules: a two-tier intervention module and an inspection scheduler. The intervention module delivers advisory-level guidance for normal executions and minor lapses, while providing replacement-level guidance for severe misbehaviors.
