Driven by the intervention level, the scheduler adjusts the inspection frequency by tightening supervision after replacement-level guidance and relaxing it after advisory-level guidance. Evaluated on 300 SWE-bench Lite tasks across two distinct agents, mini-swe-agent and Moatless, Cadence achieves the highest resolve rate among all evaluated monitors. Specifically, Cadence outperforms vanilla agents by 25.33\% (+76 resolved tasks) on mini-swe-agent and 15.67\% (+47 resolved tasks) on Moatless, while maintaining competitive token efficiency compared to state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [33] [TestPrism: Rethinking Test Evaluation Beyond a Single Reference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12289)
*Han Li,Lingxiang Hu,Jiacheng Huang,Ziqian Jiang,Jingkai Luo,Wei Gao,Yunfan Tan,Zun Wang,Jiaheng Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出TestPrism基准与Joint Success Function指标，揭示当前LLM编码代理测试生成被单一参考解高估的问题，并提出TestHelix方法显著提升测试质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM编码代理在测试生成上进展显著，但普遍采用单一参考解评估测试，忽略了其他有效实现，导致测试质量被高估。

Method: 构建TestPrism，含来自17个来源的300个测试任务与3000个候选实现（有效与无效对半）；提出Joint Success Function指标，要求测试在初始程序状态失败、接受所有有效候选、拒绝所有无效候选。进一步提出TestHelix，融合测试-修复对的异构合成、同伴交叉验证与递归自我改进（RSI）。

Result: 在十四种基线编码代理配置中，Joint Success Function仅达28.00%，而单一参考解成功率为59.67%。问题包括遗漏行为、不支持的断言及错误测试构造。TestHelix在两个模型上较原生harness对照提升8.67至9.00个百分点。

Conclusion: 单一参考解评估显著高估测试质量，Joint Success Function提供更严格评估；TestHelix通过异构合成、交叉验证与RSI有效改进测试生成。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) coding agents have advanced test generation across diverse programming tasks. However, the common practice of evaluating tests against a single reference solution overlooks alternative valid implementations and can overstate test quality. We introduce TestPrism, comprising 300 test tasks from 17 sources and 3000 candidate implementations, evenly split between valid and invalid solutions. Its primary metric, Joint Success Function, requires the generated tests to fail on the initial program state, accept every valid candidate, and reject every invalid candidate. Across fourteen baseline coding agent configurations, Joint Success Function reaches only 28.00%, whereas single reference success reaches 59.67%. Our analysis reveals missed behaviors, unsupported assertions, and faulty test construction. To address these weaknesses, we introduce TestHelix, which combines heterogeneous synthesis of test and repair pairs with peer cross validation and recursive self improvement (RSI). Across two models, TestHelix improves Joint Success Function by 8.67 to 9.00 percentage points over the native harness comparators in the TestHelix evaluation

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [34] [LIFT: A Lifecycle-aware Interaction Factorization Transformer for Unified Retrieval and Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10556)
*Keji Miao,Enhao Cheng,Yan Li,Qun Li,Qi Zhang,Jie Yuan,Xiaoyong Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出LIFT模型，将交互建模为请求-物品-上下文-动作的因果序列，实现检索与排序阶段共享历史建模同时保留阶段特定信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 级联推荐系统中检索与排序使用相同的用户历史，但两阶段在交互的不同时点获取不同信息，现有方法未能很好地区分和利用这种阶段差异。

Method: 将每次交互分解为有序的Request、Item、Context、Action状态并建模为因果序列；检索读取Request状态，排序读取Context状态；用Role-Conditioned Attention和轻量级Pre-LN Bias实现该表示。

Result: 在ML-20M和Taobao上，LIFT在联合模型中取得最高Joint Score，较最强基线分别提升4.9%和3.6%；损失权重扫描显示良好的检索-排序权衡，消融和缩放分析进一步验证了序列构建、模型组件与容量设置。

Conclusion: LIFT通过生命周期感知的序列分解与角色条件建模，统一了检索与排序的历史建模并保留各阶段信息，在联合效果上优于基线，具备良好的阶段权衡与可扩展性。

Abstract: Cascaded recommender systems use the same user history for retrieval and ranking, while the two stages have access to different information at different points of an interaction. We propose the Lifecycle-aware Interaction Factorization Transformer (LIFT), which decomposes each interaction into ordered Request, Item, Context, and Action states and models them as a causal sequence. Retrieval reads the Request state, while ranking reads the Context state, allowing both tasks to share history modeling while preserving stage-specific information. LIFT instantiates this representation with Role-Conditioned Attention and a lightweight Pre-LN Bias. On ML-20M and Taobao, LIFT achieves the highest Joint Score among the evaluated joint models, improving over the strongest baselines by 4.9% and 3.6%, respectively. Loss-weight sweeps show favorable retrieval--ranking trade-offs, while ablations and scaling analyses further examine lifecycle sequence construction, model components, and capacity settings.

</details>


### [35] [Learning Multi-Step Query Rewriting via Corpus Feedback for Conversational Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10955)
*João Coelho,Hong Wang,Jie Yuan,Zhuoer Wang,Samson Koelle,Wei Niu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文将对话查询重写（CQR）重构为序贯检索问题：智能体先重写当前轮次并检索，再依据检索到的段落条件化地生成下一次重写，在由三类重写操作加停止动作构成的类型化空间内行动，通过监督微调加检索质量奖励的强化学习训练，无需人工重写标注。在TopiOCQA与QReCC上优于多个检索对齐基线，跨检索后端有效，并零训练泛化至CAsT；仅凭检索奖励优化即自发涌现出将伪文档锚定于早期检索段落的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CQR方法多为"单步"范式：仅依据对话历史一次性生成重写查询再检索，重写在获得任何语料证据之前即被固定，无法依据检索结果纠正指代消解或词汇选择，导致错误无法回修。

Method: 将CQR重铸为序贯检索（sequential retrieval）问题：构建一个智能体，对当前轮次重写→检索→以检索返回段落为条件进行下一次重写，循环直到停止。动作空间为类型化的三类重写操作：1) 消解对话意图为独立查询；2) 生成词汇层面的改写（lexical reformulation）；3) 合成伪文档以进行文档到文档（doc-to-doc）匹配；外加一个终止episode的停止动作。训练采用监督微调（SFT）后接强化学习（RL），仅使用单一的检索质量奖励，不使用人工重写标注。

Result: 在TopiOCQA与QReCC两个数据集上，该智能体优于若干检索对齐（retrieval-aligned）基线；在不同检索后端（retrieval backends）上均保持有效；无需额外训练即可泛化至CAsT基准。分析表明，仅通过检索奖励优化，学到的策略自发形成"将伪文档锚定于前序步骤检索到的段落"的行为，显著提升检索效果。

Conclusion: 将对话查询重写从单步生成改为以检索证据为反馈的序贯决策过程是有效的；类型化重写动作空间与纯检索质量奖励能在无人工重写标注下驱动策略学习，并涌现出伪文档锚定行为。方法具备跨后端鲁棒性与零样本跨基准泛化能力，表明检索奖励本身可作为CQR的有效监督信号。

Abstract: Conversational Query Rewriting (CQR) turns a context dependent user turn into a standalone query for a retriever, and most methods do this in a single step from the dialogue history before retrieving once. The rewrite is therefore fixed before any corpus evidence is available to correct its reference resolution or its vocabulary. We recast CQR as a sequential retrieval problem: an agent rewrites the current turn, retrieves, and conditions its next rewrite on the returned passages. The agent acts in a typed space of three rewriting operations, resolving conversational intent into a standalone query, generating lexical reformulations, or synthesizing pseudo-documents for document-to-document matching, together with a stop action that ends the episode. We train the policy with supervised fine-tuning followed by reinforcement learning against a single retrieval-quality reward, using no human rewrite annotations. Across TopiOCQA and QReCC, the agent outperforms several retrieval-aligned baselines, while remaining effective across retrieval backends and generalizing to the CAsT benchmarks without additional training. Further analysis shows that, through retrieval-reward optimization alone, the learned policy develops a behavior of grounding pseudo-documents in passages retrieved by earlier steps, substantially improving retrieval.

</details>


### [36] [H2CE: Modeling Geo-Semantic Interactions for POI Reranking with Heterogeneous Two-Stage Cross-Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11277)
*Zhengwei Bai,Moreno D'Incà,Danielle Class,Alessandro Moschitti*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出H2CE异构两阶段交叉编码器，用于时延受限的POI重排序，兼顾语义、地理与数值质量信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本地搜索中POI重排序需在语义、地理邻近性与评分/评论数等数值质量信号间进行查询条件化的权衡；近距离POI可能仅部分满足意图，而较远POI可能具备更强的语义与质量证据，同时需满足实时服务约束。

Method: H2CE以两种互补方式表示数值属性：将分桶后的自然语言描述插入交叉编码器输入以支持语义-数值注意力，同时用专用MLP处理精确标量值以保留量级信息；语义与数值嵌入经隐空间聚合融合，实现超越标量加权和的非线性交互。架构为两阶段：阶段1对所有候选项逐点打分以可扩展过滤，阶段2对Top-K候选进行成对比较并用Copeland聚合，将成对代价从O(N^2)降至O(N+K(K-1))。

Result: 在5,743条查询的本地搜索测试集上，H2CE达到67.48% NDCG@5，较XGBoost LTR绝对提升+22.82%，较零样本LLM重排序器提升+35.89%；成对阶段较仅逐点模型额外提升+1.98% NDCG@5。消融验证了数值特征、隐空间聚合、Top-K成对重排序与对齐训练的价值。

Conclusion: H2CE通过异构数值表示、隐空间融合与两阶段（逐点+成对Copeland）架构，在时延约束下显著提升POI重排序效果。

Abstract: Point-of-Interest (POI) reranking in local search must model query-conditioned tradeoffs among lexical semantics, geospatial proximity, and numerical quality signals such as rating and review count, while remaining practical under real-time serving constraints. A close POI may only partially satisfy the query intent, while a farther one may offer stronger semantic and quality evidence. We present H2CE, a Heterogeneous Two-stage Cross-Encoder for latency-bounded POI reranking. H2CE represents numerical attributes in two complementary ways: bucketized natural-language descriptors are inserted into the cross-encoder input to support semantic--numeric attention, while exact scalar values are processed by dedicated MLPs to preserve magnitude information. The resulting semantic and numerical embeddings are fused through latent-space aggregation, enabling nonlinear interactions beyond scalar weighted sums. H2CE then applies a two-stage architecture: Stage 1 scores all candidates pointwise for scalable filtering, and Stage 2 performs head-to-head pairwise comparison among the top-$K$ candidates with Copeland aggregation, making fine-grained relative tradeoffs explicit while reducing pairwise cost from O(N^2) to O(N+K(K-1)). On a 5,743-query local search test set, H2CE achieves 67.48% NDCG@5, improving over XGBoost LTR by +22.82% absolute and over a zero-shot LLM reranker by +35.89%. The pairwise stage adds +1.98% NDCG@5 over the pointwise model alone. Ablations confirm the value of numerical features, latent aggregation, top-K pairwise reranking, and aligned training.

</details>


### [37] [EVIE: Evidence-Vector-Informed Embeddings for Visual Document Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11553)
*Zifei Wang,Wei Wen,Qiang Ji,Qian-Wen Zhang,Ruizhi Qiao,Xing Sun*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 EVIE，一种原生视觉文档检索器家族，通过证据引导的数据治理、双向师生蒸馏与 Prefix-MRL、以及层次凝聚索引压缩，在精度和存储之间取得更优平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉文档检索方法难以兼顾细粒度页面理解与高效索引：基于 OCR 的文本检索有预处理延迟且丢失视觉结构线索；单向量视觉语言模型压缩整个页面为一个向量，匹配粒度受限；多向量 MaxSim 检索器交互更细但索引庞大且精度仍有提升空间。作者认为需在表示学习和索引构建全过程中保留与查询相关的页面证据。

Method: EVIE 集成三项创新：(1) 证据判定数据治理，用多模态裁判识别含答案的正样本并过滤不可靠负样本；(2) 双向师生学习，结合对称 listwise 蒸馏与基于前缀的 Matryoshka 表示学习（Prefix-MRL），使单一学生检查点支持六种嵌套嵌入维度而无需重编码；(3) 层次凝聚索引压缩（HAC），对页面 token 进行带空间正则的聚类并存储语义质心，用于单阶段 MaxSim 检索。

Result: 在 ViDoRe V1、V2、V3 和 JinaVDR 的 138 项任务上验证：EVIE-8B 在 V3 上达 66.75 nDCG@10，超过最佳外部基线 1.43 分，四套件平均 79.51；EVIE-4.5B 配 HAC 在每百万页仅 3.81 GiB 下保持 59.58 nDCG@10，向量负载减少 128 倍。

Conclusion: EVIE 通过证据向量引导的表示与索引设计，改善了视觉文档检索的精度—存储权衡。

Abstract: Accurate and scalable visual document retrieval (VDR) requires both fine-grained page understanding and efficient indexing, yet existing approaches struggle to achieve both. OCR-based text retrieval adds preprocessing latency and can lose visual and structural cues needed to understand complex pages. Single-vector vision-language models bypass OCR, but compressing an entire page into one vector limits the granularity of query--document matching. Multi-vector retrievers with MaxSim provide finer interactions, yet demand large indexes and still leave room for accuracy improvements. We argue that overcoming these limitations requires preserving query-relevant page evidence throughout representation learning and index construction. To this end, we introduce \textbf{\textit{EVIE}} (Evidence-Vector-Informed Embeddings), a family of native visual document retrievers integrating three key innovations: (1) Evidence-judged data governance, which uses a multimodal judge to identify answer-bearing positives and filter unreliable negatives. (2) Bidirectional teacher--student learning with symmetric listwise distillation and prefix-based Matryoshka representation learning (Prefix-MRL), enabling one student checkpoint to serve six nested embedding dimensions without re-encoding. (3) Hierarchical agglomerative index compression (HAC), which clusters page tokens with spatial regularization and stores semantic centroids for single-stage MaxSim retrieval. Extensive experiments across 138 tasks from ViDoRe V1, V2, V3, and JinaVDR validate EVIE. EVIE-8B achieves 66.75 nDCG@10 on V3, exceeding the best external baseline by 1.43 points, with a four-suite average of 79.51. EVIE-4.5B with HAC retains 59.58 nDCG@10 at only 3.81 GiB per million pages, reducing vector payload by $128\times$. Together, these results improve the accuracy--storage trade-off for visual document retrieval.

</details>


### [38] [Overview of the NTCIR-19 Automatic Evaluation of LLMs 2 (AEOLLM-2) Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11598)
*Junjie Chen,Yuxi Dong,Haitao Li,Yiqun Liu,Qingyao Ai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文概述了NTCIR-19的AEOLLM-2任务，聚焦LLM长文本生成的自动评估，新增"深度研究报告评估"子任务，共收到10支团队91个提交，通过对比人工标注真值衡量各方法性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在NTCIR-18核心任务AEOLLM成功基础上，进一步探究大语言模型（LLM）的自动评估方法，尤其是长文本生成场景下的评估难题。

Method: 提出AEOLLM-2任务，新增深度研究报告评估子任务，参赛者开发自动评估方法对LLM生成的长篇深度研究报告质量进行打分，并以人工标注的真值标签为基准衡量方法性能。

Result: 本届共收到来自10支团队的91个运行结果（runs）。

Conclusion: 论文系统介绍了任务背景、数据集构建、评估指标、参赛方法与最终评估结果，为LLM长文本自动评估研究提供了基准与参考。

Abstract: In this paper, we provide an overview of the NTCIR-19 Automatic Evaluation of LLMs 2 (AEOLLM-2) task. Building on the success of the NTCIR-18 core task AEOLLM, we proposed AEOLLM-2 for NTCIR-19 to further investigate automatic evaluation methods for Large Language Models (LLMs), particularly in long-form text generation scenarios. In AEOLLM-2, we introduced a new subtask, Deep Research Evaluation, which focuses on the automatic evaluation of long-form deep research reports generated by LLMs. Participants developed evaluation methods to automatically assess the quality of these reports, and the performance of each method was measured by comparing its scores against human-annotated ground-truth labels. This year, we received 91 runs from 10 teams in total. This paper describes the background of the task, the dataset construction, the evaluation measures, the participants' methods, and the final evaluation results.

</details>


### [39] [SkillContrast: Difference-Guided Text Selection for Agent Skill Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11650)
*Jiandong Ding,Honglei Ji,Ming Liu,Tao Duan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出免训练选择器 SkillContrast，通过对比检索到的相近技能、保留差异文本及局部上下文，为预训练重排器提供紧凑输入，在 SameCapRisk-Bench 上优于 TF-IDF 查询选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相似 agent 技能可共享指令，但在使用条件上存在差异；基于查询的文本选择可能保留共享指令却忽略这些差异，导致检索到有帮助技能的同时也命中其标为高风险的"孪生"技能。

Method: SkillContrast 免训练，对比检索到的技能并保留下与其局部上下文的差异文本，交由预训练 reranker 处理；通过等长组件替换消融识别差异文本的贡献。

Result: 在 SameCapRisk-Bench 的 1,235 个请求上，相同单候选输入长度下比 TF-IDF 查询选择多出 54-72 个 clean hits（跨 2 种检索器与 2 种 reranker 规模）；相比完整技能体减少 51.1-58.8% 模型输入 token，0.6B 时少 10-18 个 clean hits，4B 时持平或更高。等长消融表明差异文本是主要贡献因素，上下文效应较小且混合。

Conclusion: 候选相对差异可补充查询相关性，用于选择紧凑的重排输入，在保持或提升检索质量的同时显著降低 token 消耗。

Abstract: Similar agent skills can share instructions but differ in their conditions of use. Query-based text selection may retain shared instructions and omit these distinctions. We introduce SkillContrast, a training-free selector that compares retrieved skills and retains their differing text with local context for a pretrained reranker. On 1,235 requests from SameCapRisk-Bench, it yields 54-72 more clean hits (requests that retrieve a helpful skill without its marked risky sibling) than TF-IDF query selection at identical per-candidate input lengths, across 2 retrievers and 2 reranker sizes. Length-matched component replacements identify differing text as the main contributor in the primary setting, with smaller, mixed context effects. Relative to full skill bodies, SkillContrast uses 51.1-58.8% fewer model-input tokens, with 10-18 fewer clean hits at 0.6B and matching or higher observed clean-hit counts at 4B. Candidate-relative differences thus complement query relevance in selecting compact reranking inputs.

</details>


### [40] [Autoregressive Retriever: Improving Query Understanding from Item Feedback for Universal Multimodal Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11666)
*Jianfei Zhao,Yifan Wang,Feng Zhang,Xin Sun,Chong Feng,Zhixing Tan,Yang Luo,Boyuan Pan,Xu Kai,Yao Hu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出自回归检索器（ARR），在检索过程中利用反馈项迭代更新查询嵌入，并通过SFT与RL训练，在域内与零样本基准上平均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统通用多模态检索对查询仅编码一次，随后依据嵌入相似度独立排序，检索效率高但查询表示保持固定；即使已检索到的条目有助于澄清信息需求，查询也无法据此调整，形成查询表示的静态性局限。

Method: ARR 在"检索一个条目"与"更新查询嵌入"之间交替进行，并用最终嵌入完成集合排序。监督微调通过逐步对比监督教编码器利用反馈；强化学习将反馈条目的选择视为动作，用相关条目最终倒数排名（reciprocal rank）优化其选择；查询侧适配器使该优化可面向固定的条目索引进行。

Result: ARR 在域内与零样本基准上均取得较强检索性能，平均超过所比较的基线；分析表明推理阶段的反馈能提升检索效果，且带反馈的训练也能在未观察任何条目之前改善初始查询嵌入。

Conclusion: 将检索与查询表示更新耦合的自回归反馈机制可有效提升多模态检索性能，并同时受益于监督与强化学习训练；反馈既在推理时起作用，也反哺初始查询编码质量。

Abstract: Universal multimodal retrieval typically encodes a query once and ranks independently indexed items by embedding similarity. This design supports efficient search, but leaves the query representation unchanged even when retrieved items could help clarify the information need. We introduce the AutoRegressive Retriever (ARR), a multimodal retrieval model that learns both to select informative items and to use their content to refine subsequent retrieval. ARR alternates between retrieving an item and updating the query embedding, then uses the final embedding to rank the collection. Supervised fine-tuning teaches the encoder to use feedback through stepwise contrastive supervision. Reinforcement learning treats feedback items as actions and optimizes their selection using the final reciprocal rank of a relevant item. A query-side adapter enables this optimization against a fixed item index. ARR demonstrates strong retrieval performance on both in-domain and zero-shot benchmarks, outperforming the compared baselines on average. Further analyses show that feedback improves retrieval at inference time and that training with feedback also improves the initial query embedding, before any item is observed.

</details>


### [41] [Chaos in the Text: Revealing the Modality Preference in Mixed-Modality Retrievers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11816)
*Yubo Sun,Chunyi Peng,Yukun Yan,Zhenghao Liu,Zhipeng Xu,Sen Mei,Linlin Xin,Zheni Zeng,Maosong Sun*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了稠密检索器在混合模态语料（文本、图像、文本-图像融合文档）上的表现，发现性能对模态组成高度敏感，呈V型曲线，并将文本表示获得系统性更高相似度导致的偏差命名为"Chaos in the Text"；提出Trident方法以缓解该偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密检索器在纯文本与纯图像语料上已取得显著进展，但其能力能否可靠迁移到同时包含文本、图像及融合文档的混合语料尚不明确，需要系统性考察其在不同模态组成下的鲁棒性。

Method: 系统评估不同架构的检索器在混合模态语料下的性能；分析模态偏好现象。提出Trident：为每个文档构建文本、图像、文本-图像融合三种共等正例视图，通过Multi-Positive View InfoNCE联合优化相关性判别与正例视图平衡。

Result: 检索性能随图像文档被语义对应文本逐步替换呈明显V型曲线：单模态语料表现强，模态共存时显著下降；无关文本比等量无关图像造成更严重退化。Trident在视觉文档与自然图像基准上，于CLIP类与VLM类架构均提升混合模态检索性能，降低对模态组成及文本干扰项的敏感性，并提升单模态检索平均性能。

Conclusion: 混合模态检索器存在显著的模态组成敏感性及文本偏置问题；Trident通过多正例视图平衡有效缓解该偏差，提升混合及单模态检索的鲁棒性与整体性能。

Abstract: Dense retrievers have made significant progress on text and image corpora, but whether these capabilities extend reliably to mixed corpora containing text, image, and fused text-image documents remains unclear. In this paper, we systematically examine retrievers across architectures and find that their performance is highly sensitive to modality composition. As image documents are progressively replaced with semantically corresponding text representations, retrieval performance follows a pronounced V-shaped curve, remaining strong on single-modality corpora but degrading substantially when modalities coexist. In particular, irrelevant text causes more severe degradation than an equal number of irrelevant images, a phenomenon we term Chaos in the Text. Further analysis reveals modality preference, whereby text representations receive systematically higher similarity scores, allowing irrelevant text to outrank relevant images. To mitigate this bias, we introduce Trident, which constructs text, image, and fused text-image views of each document as co-equal positives and jointly optimizes relevance discrimination and positive-view balance through Multi-Positive View InfoNCE. Experiments across visual document and natural image benchmarks show that trident improves mixed-modality retrieval on both CLIP-based and VLM-based architectures, reduces sensitivity to modality composition and text distractors, and increases average single-modality retrieval performance.

</details>


### [42] [Project Greenhouse: Progress Toward Fully Open and Sovereign Agentic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11922)
*Jimmy Lin,Sahel Sharifymoghaddam,Lingwei Gu,Nour Jedidi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该文介绍"Project Greenhouse"，论证仅用有限算力即可构建完全开放、自主可控的智能体检索模型，并以从零预训练+有监督微调的两步配方训练出具有竞争力的pointwise解码器式重排序器（Gaggle系列），全程不依赖第三方开放权重骨干。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流做法依赖第三方开放权重骨干模型，导致训练过程不完全可控、开放性与自主性受限。作者试图验证：以适度计算资源和常见公开数据集，能否端到端自主训练出面向智能体检索的、完全开放且主权可控的模型。

Method: 采用简单的两步配方：从零开始的预训练（pre-training from scratch），随后进行有监督微调（supervised fine-tuning）；仅使用常见公开数据集，不使用任何第三方开放权重骨干，从而完全掌控模型训练全流程。实验主要借助少量GPU完成。

Result: 成功构建出具有竞争力的pointwise解码器式重排序器（decoder-only reranker）；大部分实验仅用不超过少量GPU即可完成；发布Gaggle系列模型检查点，以及数据、代码与配置，支持透明、独立的复现。

Conclusion: 证明了以有限计算资源从零训练完全开放、自主可控的智能体检索模型的可行性，是验证其更广泛论题的第一步，并为透明复现提供了完整工件。

Abstract: Project Greenhouse represents our exploration of a simple thesis: We believe that it is possible to build fully open and sovereign models for agentic search with only modest computational resources. As a first milestone, we describe how to build a competitive pointwise decoder-only reranker using a simple two-step recipe comprising pre-training from scratch followed by supervised fine-tuning, starting only from commonly available datasets. Contrary to the dominant approach in the literature, we do not rely on existing open-weight backbones from third parties, and thus we are fully in control of model training, from end to end. We were able to accomplish the bulk of our experiments using no more than a handful of GPUs. This report articulates the importance and benefits of our approach, and we share artifacts that enable transparent, independent reproduction of all aspects of model training. Beyond data, code, and configurations that capture our efforts, we also release checkpoints for our family of Gaggle models, demonstrating the feasibility of our approach and providing a first step toward validating our broader thesis.

</details>


### [43] [NativeScope: Relation-Localized Retrieval over Native Topology with a Correct Anchor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12243)
*Long Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 NativeScope 的"先定范围后排序"检索方法，利用数据固有结构（章节、会话边界、原生顺序）将查询建模为 q -> (A, r, B)，在文档与记忆记录上显著优于整体式 Dense RAG，但硬性范围约束会继承定位误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密检索通常仅依据文本块与问题的语义相似度排序，忽略了数据系统中已存在的结构信息，如章节归属、会话边界与原生顺序，导致检索缺乏精确的范围约束。

Method: 将查询表示为 q -> (A, r, B)：锚点 A 与关系 r 通过 belonging、before、after 算子选出原生单元，目标词 B 仅对与所选范围重叠的文本块排序。同时设置内部变体 NS-FullQ，用完整问题对同一批候选进行排序。在 QASPER 与 LongMemEval 衍生的 200 条受控文档与记忆记录上、1,024 token 预算下评估两种方法。

Result: NativeScope 在文档上达到 89.28% 的原生单元召回，在记忆上为 72.50%，较实例级 Dense RAG 分别提升 42.75 与 22.00 个百分点；NS-FullQ 为 87.78% 与 68.50%，其与 NativeScope 的差异不具结论性，说明主要增益来自关系定范围而非更短的排序查询。在自动 Top-1 锚点下，记忆召回降至 35.50%。

Conclusion: 当锚点坐标与原生关系可靠时，NativeScope 效果显著；其收益主要源于关系式范围定位。但硬性范围约束会继承定位接口的误差，自动锚点下性能明显下降，说明方法对定位精度高度敏感。

Abstract: Dense retrieval usually ranks text chunks by their semantic similarity to a question. This ignores structure that many data systems already store, including section membership, session boundaries, and native order. We propose NativeScope, a scope-then-rank method for queries with a known anchor and relation. It represents a query as q -> (A, r, B). The anchor A and relation r select native units through belonging, before, or after operators, and the target term B ranks only chunks that overlap the selected scope. An internal variant, NS-FullQ, ranks the same candidates with the full question. We evaluate both methods on 200 controlled document and memory records derived from QASPER and LongMemEval under a 1,024-token budget. NativeScope attains native-unit recall of 89.28 percent for documents and 72.50 percent for memories, improving over instance-wide Dense RAG by 42.75 and 22.00 percentage points. NS-FullQ reaches 87.78 percent and 68.50 percent; its differences from NativeScope are inconclusive, locating the primary gain in relational scoping rather than the shorter ranking query. With automatic Top-1 anchors, memory recall falls to 35.50 percent. NativeScope is therefore effective when anchor coordinates and native relations are reliable, but hard scoping inherits errors from the localization interface.

</details>


### [44] [Compact and Efficient Indexes for Learned Sparse Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12300)
*Franco Maria Nardini,Luca Rizzo,Cosimo Rulli,Rossano Venturini*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出基于SEISMIC的稀疏检索索引内存优化方案：倒排索引用medoid替代块摘要，前向索引通过词表重排、DOTPACKING8位压缩与4位码本量化压缩，并引入JUMPDOT点积核，在同等精度下实现最高5.3倍加速与约3倍内存节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习型稀疏检索索引（如SEISMIC）虽检索效率高，但内存占用较大。作者希望在保持主流检索数据结构效率的前提下，大幅降低索引的内存开销。

Method: 从SEISMIC的两层设计入手：倒排索引层面，用medoid（从现有文档中选出的块代表）替代昂贵的逐块摘要，将块级元数据从稀疏向量压缩为单个文档标识符；前向索引层面，同时压缩组件与取值，重排词表使共现组件相邻，并用SIMD友好的DOTPACKING8对Δ-gap编码以融合解压与点积计算，取值则采用按组件分布拟合的紧凑4位码本量化；此外提出面向少量非零查询项的块状点积核JUMPDOT。该前向索引压缩方法独立于SEISMIC，可接入任何基于前向索引打分的系统（如KANNOLO）。

Result: 在MS MARCO上使用三种主流学习型稀疏编码器评测：同等精度下，本方法索引比最优竞争者查询速度快至5.3倍、内存少约3倍；在内存最受限的场景下，仍快至1.9倍且内存少至3.9倍。

Conclusion: 通过倒排索引的medoid摘要与前向索引的联合压缩（词表重排、DOTPACKING8、4位码本、JUMPDOT），显著改善了学习型稀疏检索的速度-空间权衡，且其前向索引压缩具有通用可插拔性。

Abstract: This paper investigates how to substantially reduce the memory footprint of learned sparse retrieval indexes without sacrificing the efficiency of state-of-the-art retrieval data structures. Building on SEISMIC, we revisit both levels of its design: the inverted index used to select candidates and the forward index used to score them. For the inverted index, we replace costly per-block summaries with medoids, namely existing documents elected as block representatives, collapsing the per-block metadata from a sparse vector to a single document identifier. For the forward index, we compress both components and values. We reorder the vocabulary to place co-occurring components closer together and encode the resulting $Δ$-gaps with DOTPACKING8, a SIMD-friendly bit-packing scheme that fuses decompression with dot-product evaluation; values are quantized with compact per-component 4-bit codebooks fitted to each component's distribution. We further introduce JUMPDOT, a blocked dot-product kernel tailored for queries that contain only a few non-zero entries. Our forward-index compression is independent of SEISMIC and can be plugged into any system relying on forward-index-based scoring, as we demonstrate by integrating it into KANNOLO. A comprehensive evaluation on MS MARCO with three state-of-the-art learned sparse encoders shows that our solutions markedly improve the speed-space trade-off of learned sparse retrieval: at equal accuracy, our indexes answer queries up to 5.3x faster than the best competitor while using about 3x less memory, and in the most memory-constrained regime, they remain up to 1.9x faster while using up to 3.9x less memory.

</details>

### [45] [Freeze the Decoder, Heal the Encoder: Parameter-Efficient Adaptation for SVD-Based KV-Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10552)
*Yufeng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出在比较参数高效微调方案时使用统一学习率会造成混淆偏差：可训练参数量差异大的各方案在同一学习率下，会人为放大最小参数量方案的优势。作者在SVD后处理的KV-cache压缩场景中验证了这一现象，并证明在逐方案调参后，仅训练编码器的"疗愈"方案与完整训练达到持平，同时节省3倍可训练参数与3倍优化器状态内存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数高效微调（PEFT）方案对比中，常以单一共享学习率进行多方案比较。但当各方案可训练参数量差异显著时，共享学习率会压低参数量较大方案的性能均值并抬高其方差，从而制造出最小参数量方案"多随机种子显著"的虚假优势。作者希望揭示并纠正这一混淆，为低秩KV-cache压缩的微调方案提供可靠的比较与实用配方。

Method: 在一具体场景中记录该混淆：对已预训练模型做SVD后处理的KV-cache压缩，将key/value权重分解为降维投影（encoder）与升维投影（decoder），再进行短时微调（healing）以恢复截断造成的精度损失。对比三类疗愈方案：仅训练encoder、仅训练decoder、同时训练两者。首先在共享学习率下比较，然后为每个方案单独调优学习率，并在视觉语言模型Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct上以每个配置三个随机种子验证，再在纯文本测试平台、两个骨干网络上进行复现。

Result: 共享学习率下，冻结decoder、仅healing encoder看似显著优于healing decoder或两者兼顾；一旦为每个方案单独调优学习率，这一表面优势消失，仅encoder疗愈与其它方案达到性能持平，并实现3倍更少可训练参数与3倍更少优化器状态内存的实际节省。该结论在Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct（单一压缩比协议）上以逐方案学习率调优与每配置三种子验证，并在纯文本测试平台的两种骨干网络上成功复现。

Conclusion: 仅训练encoder的疗愈是一种低内存、即插即用的低秩KV-cache压缩训练期改造方案。论文记录并纠正的"共享学习率陷阱"对于任何比较可训练参数量不同的微调方案的实验都具有警示意义：必须为每个方案单独调优学习率，否则结论可能被参数量差异所混淆。

Abstract: Comparing parameter-efficient fine-tuning recipes under a single, shared learning rate is a common but flawed practice: when the arms being compared have very different trainable-parameter counts, a shared rate can simultaneously depress the larger arms' means and inflate their variance, manufacturing a large, seemingly multi-seed-significant advantage for the smallest arm that is not a real effect. We document this confound in a concrete setting: post-hoc SVD-based KV-cache compression, where an already-pretrained model is converted to a low-rank (multi-head-latent-attention-style) cache by factorizing its key/value weights into a down-projection ("encoder") and an up-projection ("decoder"), after which a short fine-tune ("healing") recovers the accuracy lost to truncation. Under a shared learning rate, freezing the decoder and healing only the encoder looks like a clear win over healing the decoder or both factors; once every arm is given its own tuned learning rate, that apparent advantage disappears, and encoder-only healing instead reaches parity with the alternatives, at a real, measured saving of 3x fewer trainable parameters and 3x less optimizer-state memory. We verify this parity with per-arm learning-rate tuning and three seeds per configuration on a vision-language model (Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct), at the one compression ratio this protocol covers, and replicate it on a text-only testbed across two backbones. Encoder-only healing is therefore a lower-memory drop-in recipe for retrofitting low-rank KV-cache compression at training time, and the shared-learning-rate pitfall we document and correct is a cautionary result for comparing any fine-tuning recipes whose arms differ in trainable-parameter count.

</details>


### [46] [SPERA: Spherical Prior EEG Foundation Model with Geometry- and Frequency-Aware Latent Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10571)
*Minsu Kim,Ye-Sung Kim,Hyeseong Jeon,Wooseok Hyung,Joshua Lee,Chang-Hwan Im*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPERA是一种基于JEPA架构的EEG基础模型，通过勒让德多项式空间先验、分解式时空注意力与关系谱正则器，在10.6万条数据/约8万小时EEG上预训练，于九项下游任务取得最高平均平衡准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG虽为非侵入式神经活动测量手段，但受被试、设备与电极蒙太奇异质性影响，通用EEG模型难以构建；现有EEG基础模型多采用基于观测信号的重建目标，而观测信号同时包含神经与非神经成分，导致表征学习受干扰。

Method: 提出SPERA（球形先验EEG表征架构），采用联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）在潜空间中预测；引入勒让德多项式空间先验并融入注意力以编码不同头皮电极几何；采用分解式时间与空间注意力并交错周期全注意力块；设计关系谱正则器，使潜空间相似性结构与频谱视图对齐。

Result: 在29,048名被试、106个数据集、约80,000小时EEG上预训练；在涵盖临床、认知与BCI应用的九项下游任务中取得最高平均平衡准确率；线性探测下参数效率高，且在不同记录条件下表现稳健。

Conclusion: SPERA表明潜空间预测式预训练结合空间先验与谱结构正则可有效缓解EEG异质性问题，具备作为通用EEG分析骨干的潜力。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) provides a non-invasive measure of ongoing neural activity, but building general-purpose EEG models remains challenging due to the heterogeneity of subjects, devices, and electrode montages. Existing EEG foundation models predominantly rely on reconstruction-based objectives defined on the observed signal, which contains both neural and non-neural components. We introduce SPERA (Spherical Prior EEG Representation Architecture), an EEG foundation model that adopts the joint-embedding predictive architecture (JEPA) to predict in latent space. SPERA introduces a Legendre-polynomial spatial prior, incorporated into attention to encode varying scalp electrode geometries. Two further components adapt the model to EEG: factorized temporal and spatial attention interleaved with periodic full-attention blocks, and a relational spectral regularizer aligning latent similarity structure with spectral views. Pretrained on approximately 80,000 hours of EEG from 29,048 subjects across 106 datasets, SPERA achieves the highest average balanced accuracy across nine downstream tasks spanning clinical, cognitive, and BCI applications. SPERA further exhibits strong parameter efficiency under linear probing and robustness across varying recording conditions, suggesting its potential as a general-purpose backbone for diverse EEG analyses.

</details>


### [47] [Coverage, Not Difficulty, Sets How Much Synthetic Data an Activation Probe Needs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10594)
*Ankush Checkervarty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究激活探针（activation probe）在合成对话上的学习曲线，发现探针所需样本量由被监测概念决定：高风险与有害回复约80个样本即接近饱和，指令遵循需数倍；差异根源在于不同类别样本间的迁移程度（覆盖度）而非单类难度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活探针用于监测部署中的语言模型，但训练数据来自合成对话，探针究竟需要多少样本尚无定论；尤其当生成预算有限时，应投入在"数据种类广度"还是"每类数据深度"上缺乏量化依据。作者希望刻画概念与数据需求之间的关系，并为生成预算分配提供依据。

Method: 对三个监测概念（高风险情境、对人有损害的回复、不遵循用户指令的回复），在14个留出评估分布和4个探针模型上，追踪10–590个合成样本的学习曲线，并变化生成器LLM与提示词细节。将概念所需深度定义为拟合曲线的半增益规模（half-gain size），即获得一半增益所需的样本数；进而分解方差来源（概念、分布、生成器、探针模型、提示细节），并考察各类别之间的迁移/覆盖关系。

Result: 在Gemma-3-27B-IT上，高风险与有害探针从80个样本起就与平台期相差仅百分之几，指令探针需数倍样本，该排序在三个更小探针模型及真实样本（dev集）上同样成立。方差分解显示概念与分布解释半增益规模方差的42–45%，生成器、探针模型与提示细节合计不足10%。决定半增益规模的是覆盖度（某分布饱和所需自身类别样本数）而非单类难度：每种类别（每个评估分布对应一种）在三概念下中位半增益规模均为7–11个自身类别的合成样本。差异在于跨类别迁移程度：高风险下几乎完全迁移，有害次之，指令最低，这解释了概念间在合成与真实样本上的大部分差距。

Conclusion: 广度的收益因概念而异：指令遵循下各类别互不覆盖，必须覆盖多种类别；高风险下一类即可覆盖其余类别，多类别近乎冗余。作者发布了评估套件、dev集与生成集。

Abstract: Activation probes that monitor deployed language models are trained on synthetic conversations, and how many a probe needs is open. We trace learning curves over 10-590 synthetic samples for three monitoring concepts, high-stakes situations, replies harmful to a person, and replies that do not follow the user's instruction, on fourteen held-out evaluation distributions and four probe models, varying the generator LLM and the prompt's detail. The need is set by what is monitored: probes for high-stakes and harmful are within a few hundredths of their plateau from 80 samples on Gemma-3-27B-IT, instruction probes need several times as many, and the ordering holds on three smaller probe models and on real samples (from dev set). Prior work advises spending a generation budget on breadth, more kinds of data, over depth, more of each kind. We read the depth a concept needs as the half-gain size of a fitted curve, the number of samples at which half the gain is in hand. Concept and distribution account for 42-45% of its variance, the generator, probe model, and prompt detail for under 10%. What sets the value of the half-gain size is coverage, not per-kind difficulty: the number of samples of its own kind a distribution needs to saturate. Every kind, one per evaluation distribution, has a median half-gain size of 7-11 own-kind synthetic samples under all three concepts alike. What differs is how far samples of one kind transfer to the concept's other kinds, almost fully under high-stakes, less under harmful, and least under instruction, which accounts for most of the gap between concepts on generated and real samples. Breadth therefore pays differently by concept: many kinds are necessary under instruction, where no kind covers another, and nearly redundant under high-stakes, where one kind covers the rest. We release the evaluation suites, dev sets, and generated sets.

</details>


### [48] [Temporal transformer CAN encoder with federated lightweight heads for anomaly detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10613)
*Konstantinos Gyftodimos,Kyriakos Chiotis,Elena Politi,George Dimitrakopoulos,Eirini Liotou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于时序Transformer CAN编码器与联邦轻量头的隐私保护车载网络异常检测框架，在开源数据集上实现鲁棒检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代车辆依赖大量ECU通过CAN总线持续通信，通信具有高频、结构化、重复性特点，即使时序、载荷或消息模式的细微变化也可能指示异常。这些异常（源于错误、故障或蓄意干扰）往往十分隐蔽，传统方法单独处理消息或依赖人工规则，难以有效识别。因此需要更好的方法来捕捉这些不规则性。

Method: 提出隐私保护的车载网络异常检测框架，核心为时序Transformer CAN编码器与联邦轻量头。轻量级Transformer编码器学习CAN信号随时间的演化，从而检测细微的时序与上下文异常；联邦学习机制允许多车辆或ECU协作改进共享模型，而无需交换原始CAN数据。

Result: 在开源数据集上的实验表明，该框架在保持效率与隐私的同时，提供了鲁棒的异常检测性能。

Conclusion: 联邦学习与时序序列建模的结合，可在不共享原始数据的前提下实现高效、隐私保护且鲁棒的车载网络异常检测。

Abstract: Modern vehicles rely on large numbers of Electronic Control Units (ECUs) that constantly exchange information over the Controller Area Network (CAN) bus. Due to the rapidity, structure, and repetition of this communication, even slight variations in timing, payload values, or message patterns can point to unusual activity. Whether due to errors, malfunctions, or deliberate interference, these anomalies are frequently subtle and challenging to identify with conventional methods that handle messages separately or rely on manually created rules. Motivated by this gap, we present a privacy-preserving framework for anomaly detection in in-vehicle networks, based on a Temporal Transformer CAN Encoder with Federated Lightweight Heads, to better capture these irregularities. The detection of subtle temporal and contextual anomalies is made possible by a lightweight Transformer encoder that learns how these signals evolve over time, while a federated learning mechanism enables several vehicles or ECUs to work together to improve a shared model without exchanging raw CAN data. This combination of federated learning and temporal sequence modeling provides robust anomaly detection performance while maintaining efficiency and privacy, according to experiments conducted on open-source datasets.

</details>


### [49] [When Routing Reveals Membership: Privacy Leakage from MoE Router Telemetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10616)
*Yixin Tan,Jiayang Liu,Lu Sun,Yuke Hu,Zheng Li,Rui Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MoE 模型推理时的路由遥测信息会额外泄露“样本是否被用于微调”的成员信息，路由器增强的成员推断攻击可在强输出信号基础上进一步提升攻击效果，且该泄露在不同训练方式与受限遥测下依然存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE 推理时产生的路由信息常被日志记录或暴露，用于监控、调试、负载分析和安全审计。与普通输出不同，这种遥测暴露了模型内部计算视图，由此引发隐私疑问：它能否揭示某样本是否被用于微调部署模型。

Method: 提出路由器增强的成员推断攻击：将传统输出侧信号与聚合的路由特征相结合，并使用从独立微调的影子模型中学习到的成员分类器，迁移应用于目标模型。

Result: 在三种 MoE 架构和三个数据域、共九种设置下，路由遥测均稳定优于强输出信号集成，在 1% FPR 下 TPR 提升 2.7–9.4 个百分点。泄露在全量微调、冻结路由器训练、LoRA 与指令微调中持续存在，且在仅有离散专家选择、受限遥测或单个影子模型时仍可观测。机理分析表明，泄露不依赖路由器特有的记忆：微调将成员信息引入隐表示，路由器即使参数冻结仍会暴露该信号的投影；扰动遥测只在保真度下降时才能削弱额外泄露。

Conclusion: 路由遥测会把一种运维信号转化为微调 MoE 模型额外的隐私攻击面，需要在日志记录与监控中加以重视。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models produce routing information during inference that may be logged or exposed for monitoring, debugging, load analysis, and safety auditing. Unlike ordinary model outputs, this telemetry reveals a view of the model's internal computation, raising a privacy question: can it reveal whether an example was used to fine-tune the deployed model? We introduce a router-augmented membership inference attack that combines conventional output-side signals with aggregated routing features and applies a membership classifier learned from independently fine-tuned shadow models to the target model. Across three MoE architectures and three data domains, router telemetry consistently improves membership inference over a strong output-signal ensemble, increasing TPR at 1\% FPR by 2.7--9.4 percentage points across all nine settings. The leakage persists across full fine-tuning, frozen-router training, LoRA, and instruction tuning, and remains observable with only discrete expert selections, restricted telemetry, or a single shadow model. Mechanistic analysis further shows that the leakage does not require router-specific memorization: fine-tuning introduces membership information into hidden representations, while the router exposes a projection of this signal even when its parameters are frozen. Perturbing the telemetry reduces this additional leakage only as its fidelity degrades. Our results show that router telemetry can turn an operational signal into an additional privacy surface for fine-tuned MoE models.

</details>


### [50] [Self-Organization from Constrained Geometric Radiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10621)
*Ming Lei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出闭系统中无需外驱动即可自发涌现动态秩序的“几何辐射诱导约束自组织”新范式，揭示四阶段循环与楔形吸引子，并证明相关定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于动态秩序自发涌现的范式均依赖外部能量流、温度淬火或缓慢驱动，无法解释闭系统在无外部驱动下如何自发产生动态秩序，因此需要建立新的自组织机制与理论框架。

Method: 研究耦合度量演化系统，通过数值模拟观察约束诱导的自组织过程；识别四个联合充分条件（不可逆几何视界、量子相干持续注入应力、不相容曲率间的内禀几何张力、有效涨落）；提出并证明三个定理及约束诱导自组织定理；通过系统扫描分析临界噪声阈值与辐射起始的幂律标度；在12种构型、多种噪声类型与三种几何流下验证普适性。

Result: 模拟揭示了普适四阶段循环：应力积累、超指数辐射、混沌坍缩、收敛至分形极限环；发现新型吸引子拓扑“楔形吸引子”，具有五个量子化曲率态和分形微涨落；证明了几何视界定理、几何能量耗散定理（蕴含闭系统中的波状熵演化）和辐射作为相变通道定理；建立约束诱导自组织定理，证明上述条件以概率一保证完整循环；系统扫描发现临界噪声阈值及辐射起始的幂律标度；在12种构型、多种噪声类型和三种几何流下验证了普适性。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了闭系统自组织的新范式，并在经典非线性约束与引力视界之间建立了精确的数学对偶，为无外部驱动下动态秩序的自发涌现提供了统一的理论与机制解释。

Abstract: How does dynamic order emerge spontaneously in closed systems without external driving? Existing paradigms all require external energy flows, temperature quenching, or slow driving. Here we report constraint-induced self-organization via geometric radiation in coupled metric evolution systems. Simulations reveal a universal four-stage cycle: stress accumulation, super-exponential radiation, chaotic collapse, and convergence to a fractal limit cycle, a novel attractor topology we term the wedge-shaped attractor, with five quantized curvature states and fractal micro-fluctuations. We identify four jointly sufficient conditions: an irreversible geometric horizon, persistent stress injection from quantum coherence, endogenous geometric tension between incompatible curvatures, and effective fluctuations. Their synergy triggers a critical avalanche at the horizon boundary. We prove three theorems: the Geometric Horizon Theorem, the Geometric Energy Dissipation Theorem (implying wave-like entropy evolution in closed systems), and the Radiation as Phase Transition Channel Theorem. We further establish the Constraint-Induced Self-Organization Theorem: these conditions guarantee the complete cycle with probability one. Systematic scans reveal a critical noise threshold and power-law scaling of radiation onset. We verify universality across 12 configurations, multiple noise types, and three geometric flows. This work establishes a new paradigm for closed-system self-organization, forging an exact mathematical duality between classical nonlinear constraints and gravitational horizons.

</details>


### [51] [Recurrent Self-Improvement: Dynamic Cross-Loop On-Policy Distillation for Looped Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10623)
*Yi Wang,Rui Qian,Yu Li,Haoyang Yao,Wenjie Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出跨循环在线策略蒸馏框架 LoopOPD 与动态版本 D-LoopOPD，利用 LoopLM 自身循环计算作为监督信号，无需外部教师或特权信息，提升数学推理并泛化至通用推理与代码生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoopLM 通过复用共享参数实现参数高效的推理扩展，但后训练困难：现有方法要么依赖稀疏或跨循环扩展代价高的奖励监督，要么依赖外部教师或特权信息，导致教师可用性受限、师生上下文不匹配。

Method: 提出 LoopOPD：一种跨循环在线策略蒸馏框架。以冻结的终端循环策略作为“计算特权教师”，在学生的自生成 rollout 上指导中间循环学生，提供稠密监督，无需外部教师或特权信息。并提出 Dynamic LoopOPD（D-LoopOPD），随共享参数更新持续刷新终端循环教师，实现循环自改进。同时刻画蒸馏更新在循环深度间的传播机制，推导出单次更新即可在两个循环深度同时获得局部改进的充分条件。

Result: 在 Ouro-Thinking 模型上，LoopOPD 提升数学推理性能，D-LoopOPD 通过动态教师更新带来进一步提升；且仅用数学数据训练，模型在通用推理与代码生成基准上亦有提升。

Conclusion: 循环计算可作为 LoopLM 的有效监督来源，跨循环在线策略蒸馏为实现无外部教师的循环自改进提供了可行路径；代码与模型检查点将在录用后发布。

Abstract: Looped Language Models (LoopLMs) offer a parameter efficient approach to scaling reasoning by reusing shared parameters across recurrent computation steps. Despite their promise, effective post-training of LoopLMs remains challenging. Existing approaches either provide reward based supervision that is sparse or costly to extend across loops, or rely on external teachers or privileged information, leading to limited teacher availability or teacher-student context mismatch. To address these limitations, we introduce LoopOPD, a cross-loop on-policy distillation framework that uses additional recurrent computation within a LoopLM as its own source of supervision. LoopOPD uses a frozen terminal loop policy as a compute privileged teacher for an intermediate loop student on student generated rollouts, providing dense supervision without an external teacher or privileged information. We further propose Dynamic LoopOPD (D-LoopOPD), which continually refreshes the terminal loop teacher as the shared model parameters are updated, enabling recurrent self-improvement. We characterize how distillation updates propagate across loop depths and derive sufficient conditions under which a single update yields simultaneous local improvement at both loop depths. Experiments on Ouro-Thinking models show that LoopOPD improves mathematical reasoning, while D-LoopOPD yields further gains through dynamic teacher updates. Despite being trained only on mathematical data, the resulting models also improve on general reasoning and code generation benchmarks, demonstrating that recurrent computation can serve as an effective source of supervision for LoopLMs. Our code and model checkpoints will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [52] [Exact SO(3)-Equivariant Isotropic Kernels for Rotation-Robust Neural Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10626)
*Ridham Patel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出IKNO图神经算子，通过旋转不变标量与协变矢量构建局部交互，实现三维Navier-Stokes预测的严格旋转等变性，在精度不降的前提下消除坐标依赖并大幅减少参数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矢量值PDE的神经代理模型虽能拟合训练数据，但当同一物理状态在旋转坐标系下表达时预测会改变，即缺乏旋转等变性（坐标依赖）。作者在三维Navier-Stokes、不规则采样点场景下研究并解决这一失效问题。

Method: 提出Invariant-Conditioned Isotropic Kernel Neural Operator (IKNO)：一个紧凑图模型，从旋转不变的标量量和随数据旋转的矢量方向构建局部交互，从而使预测的速度变化随输入位置与速度的旋转而精确同步旋转。并与无约束图模型、通用旋转感知Tensor Field Network（TFN）及参数匹配的图模拟器对比。

Result: 在模型设计后固定的留出测试集上：无约束图模型用随机旋转样本训练只能减少而无法消除坐标依赖；IKNO达到数值精度一致的旋转等变性，预测精度与TFN相当但参数少5.6倍，且优于参数匹配的图模拟器。

Conclusion: 紧凑、面向PDE专门设计的模型能够在不牺牲预测精度的前提下消除坐标依赖，为矢量值PDE的等变神经代理提供了高效方案。

Abstract: Neural surrogates for vector-valued partial differential equations can fit training data yet change their predictions when the same physical state is expressed in a rotated coordinate frame. We study this failure on three-dimensional Navier--Stokes dynamics observed at irregularly placed points. We introduce the Invariant-Conditioned Isotropic Kernel Neural Operator (IKNO), a compact graph model that builds local interactions from scalar quantities unchanged by rotation and vector directions that rotate with the data. Consequently, rotating the positions and velocities rotates the predicted velocity change in exactly the same way. On a held-out test set fixed after model design, training unconstrained graph models on randomly rotated examples reduces but does not eliminate their coordinate dependence. In contrast, IKNO is consistent to numerical precision, matches the forecasting accuracy of a general rotation-aware Tensor Field Network with $5.6$ times fewer parameters, and outperforms a parameter-matched graph simulator. These results show that a compact, PDE-specialized model can remove coordinate dependence without sacrificing forecasting accuracy.

</details>


### [53] [Language Models for Page-Level Layout Decisions in E-commerce Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10920)
*Varun Joshi,Eva C. Song,ChengXiang Zhai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了电商搜索页面中"二级堆叠"模块插入位置对用户参与度影响的离线评估方法，比较了基于提示词直接判断、提示词衍生特征和表征三类语言模型评估器。结果表明，基于表征的方法在预测用户参与度方面显著优于基于提示词的判断，可作为电商搜索中离线布局评估的可靠基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代电商搜索页面除传统排序结果列表外，还嵌入推荐系统模块（如在特定位置展示"二级堆叠"）。合适的放置能提升参与度，但次优放置会打断浏览流、降低主结果质量。传统排序评估有交错法等成熟手段，而页面级布局变更（何时何地插入二级堆叠）缺乏成熟离线评估方法，仅靠昂贵的在线A/B测试不切实际。因此需要研究可扩展的离线评估手段。

Method: 将语言模型作为可扩展的评估器，针对"是否在特定位置加入二级堆叠"这一布局决策，研究并对比三类离线评估方法：(1) 直接基于提示词（prompt-based）的判断；(2) 提示词衍生特征（prompt-derived feature）；(3) 基于表征（representation-based）的方法，并以预测用户参与度作为评估任务。

Result: 基于表征的方法在预测用户参与度上持续优于基于提示词的判断方法，说明其为电商搜索场景下的离线布局评估提供了可靠基础。

Conclusion: 语言模型可充当可扩展的离线布局评估器；其中基于表征的范式比基于提示词的范式更可靠，可用于在无需昂贵在线A/B测试的前提下评估页面级布局决策。

Abstract: E-commerce search pages are critical touchpoints for millions of online shoppers. While traditional search engines return a ranked list of results, modern E-commerce search pages increasingly incorporate recommender system modules -- for example, secondary stacks that surface alternative product groupings at specific positions. When introduced appropriately, secondary stacks can improve user engagement; however, suboptimal placement may disrupt browsing flow and degrade the primary results. Unlike traditional search ranking, where evaluation techniques such as interleaving are well established, evaluating page-level layout changes e.g., when and where to insert a secondary stack remains challenging without costly online A/B testing. To address this, we study offline methods for evaluating whether a given layout decision -- specifically, the inclusion of a secondary stack at a particular position -- is beneficial to users. We investigate language models as scalable evaluators by comparing direct prompt-based, prompt-derived feature, and representation-based methods. Our results show that representation-based approaches consistently outperform prompt-based judging in predicting user engagement, suggesting they provide a reliable foundation for offline layout evaluation in E-commerce search.

</details>


### [54] [Sample-Efficiency of Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10627)
*Kevin Riehl,Shaimaa K. El-Baklish,Fan Wu,Anastasios Kouvelas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究 Kolmogorov-Arnold 网络（KAN）在深度强化学习中的样本效率，基于 Feynman 数据集与 Gymnasium 基准实验表明，KAN 可用少 40% 的样本达到相当性能，训练中相对性能提升最高达 50%，且该增益对奖励噪声稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习虽优于经典控制方法，但真实应用中样本获取成本高昂，样本效率成为核心挑战；KAN 在控制问题中可高效学习物理关系，且相比 MLP 具有更高的参数效率与可解释性，因此有必要系统评估其样本效率潜力。

Method: 采用计算实验进行系统性样本效率研究，实验覆盖 Feynman 数据集与 Gymnasium 强化学习基准，对比 Kolmogorov-Arnold 架构与 MLP 架构，并考察不同奖励噪声水平下的表现稳健性。

Result: 使用 KAN 架构可用少 40% 的样本达到相近性能；训练过程中相对性能提升最高可达 50%；上述增益在不同奖励噪声水平下保持稳健。

Conclusion: Kolmogorov-Arnold 架构在实现更高样本效率的强化学习方面具有显著潜力。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning has achieved substantial performance gains over classical control approaches. Yet, a central challenge to learning in real-world applications is acquiring costly samples. Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks are a recently proposed architecture that can learn physical relationships in control problems effectively, with significantly higher parameter efficiency and interpretability when compared to Multi-Layer-Perceptron architectures. In this work, we systematically study sample-efficiency using computational experiments, covering the Feynman dataset and the Gymnasium RL benchmark. The results show that similar performance can be achieved with 40% fewer samples using the Kolmogorov-Arnold architecture, and that relative performance improvements up to 50% occur during the training process. The observed gains are robust to varying levels of noise in rewards. These results highlight the potential of the Kolmogorov-Arnold architectures for more sample-efficient reinforcement learning. Code: https://github.com/DerKevinRiehl/neurips26_kan_training

</details>


### [55] [Phase-HDC: Replacing Optimizer History with Gradient Thresholds in Discrete Phase Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10630)
*Ahmed Nebli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Phase-HDC 提出一种仅存储模型本身（每个参数为低比特角度）的训练方法：每次更新最多将角度移动一步，方向取当前梯度符号，且仅当梯度足够大时执行。该方法可视为带固定变更代价的一阶损失模型的精确解，在保持与 Adam 相当精度的同时大幅降低优化器状态内存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练紧凑模型的显存需求远大于存储模型本身，因为优化器需保存梯度历史（如 Adam 的一阶/二阶矩）。对于参数为低比特角度的超维分类器（phase memory），这些记录的内存开销是模型本身的数倍。作者希望探索能否在只存储模型、不存任何优化器状态的前提下完成训练。

Method: Phase-HDC 的更新规则：每个存储角度每次更新最多移动一步，方向取当前梯度的反号，且仅当该梯度幅度足够大时才更新。作者证明该简单规则是"每个被修改参数支付固定代价"的一阶损失模型的精确解。实验在固定其他所有设置下与 Adam 对比（含 6-bit 矩的 Adam）。

Result: 在全部条件固定时，Phase-HDC 匹配使用 6-bit 矩的 Adam 精度，而存储量少 3 倍。在 11 个图像、表格和文本数据集上，其存储量比标准 float32 Adam 少 16–23 倍，比 8-bit Adam 少 4–6 倍。代价是相对 float32 Adam 平均损失约 5 个精度点；但在 11 个数据集中的 6 个上精度超过 8-bit Adam，包括 8-bit Adam 崩溃的字节级文本预测任务。

Conclusion: 插桩训练实验表明：当参数必须位于离散网格上时，Adam 的矩主要决定参数是否移动，而这一决策可由当前梯度的阈值在无内存的情况下完成；矩的粗量化会破坏数据中罕见输入下的该决策。需注意，存储节省为逻辑状态层面的节省，而非实测硬件内存。

Abstract: Training a compact model often needs far more memory than storing it, because the optimizer keeps its own records of past gradients. For a hyperdimensional classifier whose learned parameters are low-bit angles, which we call a \emph{phase memory}, these records take several times more memory than the model itself. We ask whether such a model can be trained while storing nothing but the model. The proposed method, Phase-HDC, turns each stored angle by at most one step per update, against the sign of its current gradient, and only when that gradient is large enough. We show that this simple rule is the exact solution of a first-order loss model in which every changed parameter pays a fixed cost. When everything except the update rule is held fixed, Phase-HDC matches the accuracy of Adam with 6-bit moments while storing three times less. Across eleven image, tabular, and text datasets, it stores 16--23$\times$ less than standard float32 Adam and 4--6$\times$ less than 8-bit Adam. The price is an average loss of about five accuracy points against float32 Adam, while Phase-HDC is more accurate than 8-bit Adam on six of the eleven datasets, including byte-level text prediction, where 8-bit Adam collapses. Instrumented training runs explain these outcomes. Once parameters must sit on a discrete grid, Adam's moments mainly decide whether a parameter moves at all, a decision that a threshold on the current gradient can make without memory, and coarse quantization of the moments breaks this decision for inputs that the data rarely contain. The storage savings are logical state rather than measured hardware memory.

</details>


### [56] [Compactness and Consistency: A Conjoint Framework for Deep Graph Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11506)
*Wei Ju,Siyu Yi,Kangjie Zheng,Yifan Wang,Ziyue Qiao,Li Shen,Yongdao Zhou,Xiaochun Cao,Jiancheng Lv*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CoCo框架，通过局部-全局图卷积滤波学习鲁棒节点表示并编码为低秩紧致嵌入，结合一致性学习实现深度图聚类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN依赖局部消息传递，难以捕捉节点间全局关系；图数据中的冗余与噪声导致节点表示缺乏紧致性和鲁棒性。

Method: 利用图卷积滤波器从局部与全局两个视角学习鲁棒节点表示，将其编码为低秩紧致嵌入以去除冗余噪声并揭示内在结构；进一步设计基于紧致嵌入的一致性学习策略，促进两个视角间的知识迁移以丰富节点语义。

Result: 在多个数据集上，CoCo优于当前最先进的方法。

Conclusion: CoCo通过联合建模紧致性与一致性，有效提升深度图聚类的表示质量与聚类性能。

Abstract: Graph clustering is a fundamental task in data analysis, aiming at grouping nodes with similar characteristics in the graph into clusters. This problem has been widely explored using graph neural networks (GNNs) due to their ability to leverage node attributes and graph topology for effective cluster assignments. However, representations learned through GNNs typically struggle to capture global relationships between nodes via local message-passing mechanisms. Moreover, the redundancy and noise inherently present in graph data may easily result in node representations lacking compactness and robustness. To address these issues, we propose a conjoint framework CoCo, which captures compactness and consistency in the learned node representations for deep graph clustering. Technically, our CoCo leverages graph convolutional filters to learn robust node representations from both local and global views, and then encodes them into low-rank compact embeddings, thus effectively removing the redundancy and noise as well as uncovering the intrinsic underlying structure. To further enrich the node semantics, we develop a consistency learning strategy based on compact embeddings to facilitate knowledge transfer from the two perspectives. Our experimental results indicate that our CoCo outperforms state-of-the-art counterparts on various datasets.

</details>


### [57] [D-SLR: The Disjoint Row-Sparse plus Low-Rank Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10636)
*Vincent Szolnoky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 Disjoint Row-Sparse plus Low-Rank (D-SLR) 分解，一种截断 SVD 的闭式替代方案：将行限定为"原样存储"或"低秩拟合"二选一，在平方误差下不损失最优性，且在所有非平凡形状下参数更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵压缩重建仍以截断 SVD 为默认方法，仅用单一低秩结构近似数据。为进一步降低残差，常见做法是叠加一个与之重叠的行稀疏分量，但这类联合问题通常依赖迭代求解器和正则化参数调优，代价高且不便于直接使用。

Method: 提出 D-SLR 分解：限制每行只能被原样存储或被低秩拟合，二者互斥。算法对整个"误差—参数"权衡曲线进行打分，随后由给定的误差目标、参数预算或选择规则确定解；同时推导出与假设无关的后验误差下界，为每个解提供可计算证书，量化其他秩与存储行选择可能带来的增益。整个网格与求解共需三次 SVD，无需调参或正则化。

Result: 在平方误差下该互斥限制不带来额外代价：联合最优解可在互斥条件下以更少参数达到，且在任意非平凡秩与存储行数（形状）下均成立。存储行数为零时 D-SLR 退化为截断 SVD，故在同等代价下不会更差。合成数据与真实数据（LLM 嵌入表、网络流量、高光谱图像）实验证实了增益，并验证了所提证书的有效性。

Conclusion: D-SLR 为截断 SVD 提供了一个闭式、免调参的即插即用改进方案，在整个误差—参数权衡上给出可证书化的解，在多种真实场景中实现更好或同等性能且参数更少。

Abstract: Compressing a matrix for reconstruction still defaults to the truncated SVD, approximating the data with a single low-rank structure. It is common to reduce the residual further by adding an overlapping row-sparse component, but methods that solve this joint problem often require iterative solvers and tuning of regularization parameters. We propose the Disjoint Row-Sparse plus Low-Rank (D-SLR) decomposition, a closed-form drop-in for the truncated SVD that improves or exactly matches it. D-SLR restricts rows to either being stored verbatim or approximated by the low-rank fit, never both. Under squared error this restriction costs nothing: the joint optimum is attainable disjointly with fewer parameters at every non-trivial rank and stored row count (shape). With zero stored rows D-SLR reduces to the truncated SVD, so it never does worse at equal cost. The algorithm scores the entire error-versus-parameters tradeoff, and the solution is chosen afterwards by a supplied error target or parameter count, or by a selection rule. The grid and solution together cost three SVDs, with no tuning or regularization. We derive an assumption-free, a-posteriori lower bound on the error at every shape, giving each solution a computable certificate on the potential gain of any other choice of rank and stored rows. Experiments on synthetic and real data (LLM embedding tables, network traffic, hyperspectral images) confirm the gains and quantify the certificate.

</details>


### [58] [Visible Reasoning Is Not a Universal Optimizer: Persona- and Thinking-Dependent Effects in Analytics Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10639)
*Bhawani Shankar Leelar,Pawan Chorasiya,Davin Hill,Robert E. Tillman,Tamer Soliman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过SQL-Python(pandas)匹配基准，控制变量检验可见CoT及"推理表征匹配目标语言"假设，发现二者均无普适优势，效果依赖persona、目标语言、模型配置与内部推理设置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可见CoT常被视为通用推理指令，且"用SQL思考/用Python思考"等表征匹配建议被广泛采用，但分析代码生成同时涉及自然语言歧义、schema grounding、目标语言约束与模型特定推理行为，这些假设缺乏基于执行的受控评估。

Method: 构建查询匹配的SQL-pandas基准，交叉persona措辞、目标语言、可见CoT格式、控制前缀、直接生成与内部推理配置；通过控制消融区分推理内容效应与提示格式效应。

Result: 未发现可见CoT的普适准确率优势，也未发现推理表征与目标语言匹配带来一致性收益；效应随persona、目标、模型配置与内部推理设置而变化。

Conclusion: 推理策略应针对模型、persona、目标语言与内部推理配置联合选择，而非作为通用默认；研究提供了识别可见推理何时提升可执行生成、何时仅扰动模型行为的受控框架。

Abstract: Visible Chain-of-Thought (CoT) is often treated as a broadly useful reasoning instruction, yet analytics code generation combines natural-language ambiguity, schema grounding, target-language constraints, and model-specific inference behavior. Because the same analytics request can be expressed in two distinct target languages-SQL and Python (pandas)-this setting provides a natural test of a common but under-examined assumption: that visible reasoning is more effective when its representation matches the requested target, as in "think in SQL" or "think in Python." Together with generic instructions such as "think step-by-step," such recommendations remain insufficiently evaluated under controlled, execution-based comparisons. We study a query matched SQL-pandas benchmark that crosses persona phrasing, target language, visible-CoT format, control prefixes, direct generation, and internal-reasoning configurations. The results do not support either a universal accuracy advantage from visible CoT or a consistent benefit from matching the reasoning representation to the target language. Instead, the effects depend on the persona, target, model configuration, and internal-reasoning setting. The control ablations further distinguish effects of reasoning content from those of prompt format. These findings indicate that reasoning strategies should be selected jointly for the model, persona, target, and internal-reasoning configuration rather than adopted as universal defaults. More broadly, the study provides a controlled framework for identifying when visible reasoning improves executable generation, when it primarily perturbs model behavior, and when the internal-reasoning configuration is the more consequential factor.

</details>


### [59] [Coverage-Aware Reasoning with Medical Tokens for Diagnosis Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10641)
*Kaisong Zhang,Haotian Fang,Junmeng Zhou,Hang Lv,Yulan Pan,Yanchao Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CARing框架，用组合式语义ID表示诊断并优化多标签覆盖的推理轨迹，在MIMIC-III/IV上取得最优加权F1与top-k召回。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM用于下次就诊诊断预测时，强化学习按最终答案正确性奖励整个轨迹，但多标签场景下单一诊断奖励无法区分"重复命中"与"覆盖不同诊断"，导致策略只集中于少数正确诊断；同时LLM分词器将ICD代码切成无语义通用token，需多步解码，难以在大疾病词表上推理。

Method: CARing以组合式语义ID（SID）表示诊断：通过残差量化将本体增强的疾病语义编码为紧凑SID；经多任务对齐与推理增强训练，将SID token锚定在自然语言与纵向EHR上下文中；用覆盖奖励强化学习与多正例监督优化无序多标签预测；推理支持直接约束解码与多链推理+排名融合。

Result: 在MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV上，CARing的加权F1超过所有EHR训练基线，并在各报告截断处取得最高top-k召回，推理模式下R@30分别为46.04%和46.52%。

Conclusion: CARing通过语义ID与覆盖导向的强化学习，有效解决多标签诊断预测中的重复命中与词表推理难题，验证了其在真实EHR数据上的优越性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) offer promising potential for next-visit diagnosis prediction, owing to their ability to integrate longitudinal clinical evidence and reason over it in natural language. However, reinforcement learning for LLM reasoning commonly rewards each trajectory according to the correctness of its final answer. In next-visit diagnosis prediction, multiple diagnoses can be simultaneously valid, but independently rewarding one diagnosis per trajectory does not distinguish repeated hits from coverage of different diagnoses. The policy can therefore concentrate on a few correct diagnoses, leaving others uncovered. Meanwhile, LLM tokenizers can split ICD codes into several generic tokens with limited clinical meaning, requiring multiple decoding steps to predict each diagnosis and hindering reasoning over a large disease vocabulary. To address both challenges, we propose CARing, a framework that represents diagnoses with compositional Semantic IDs (SIDs) and optimizes reasoning trajectories for multi-label coverage. Concretely, we first encode ontology-enriched disease semantics into compact SIDs through residual quantization, and ground the resulting SID tokens in natural language and longitudinal EHR contexts through multi-task alignment and reasoning-enriched training to unlock transferable LLM reasoning. CARing further improves unordered multi-label prediction through a coverage reward for reinforcement learning and multi-positive supervision. At inference time, the model supports both efficient direct constrained decoding and multi-chain reasoning with rank fusion. On MIMIC-III and MIMIC-IV, CARing exceeds all EHR-trained baselines in weighted F1 and attains the highest top-k recall at every reported cutoff, including R@30 of 46.04% and 46.52% in reasoning mode. Our codes and logs are available at https://github.com/zmlxzyh/CARing-Codes-Logs.

</details>


### [60] [Leakage-Controlled Multimodal Learning for Diagnosis and Progression Prediction in Alzheimer's Disease Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10648)
*Akeem Temitope Otapo,Ghazaleh Khodabandelou,Zuheng Ming,Alice Othmani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出多模态多任务框架（SFCN MRI编码器+4个因果临床Transformer+共享融合+任务特定ODE-GRU），实现阿尔茨海默病纵向预测、缺失模态处理、共病建模与亚组评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿尔茨海默病预测面临就诊不规律、测量异质、模态不完整的挑战，需在统一流程中同时处理纵向预测与亚组稳健性。

Method: 采用改进SFCN编码MRI、四个因果临床Transformer、共享融合与任务特定ODE-GRU动态建模；对MRI末两层块进行微调与LoRA适配；用Task-DRO平衡任务损失、Group-CVaR约束队列与共病分层；配合分支输入控制、按受试者分组划分与经验因果性检验。

Result: ADNI/OASIS-2/MIRIAD共2649名受试者、17317次就诊：内部验证诊断、一期进展、首访一期进展AUROC为0.935±0.002、0.884±0.003、0.870±0.005；混合AUROC为0.951/0.909/0.896；下次访视MMSE MAE 1.61；最差分层诊断AUROC 0.827±0.008。OASIS-3外部验证：网络与混合诊断AUROC 0.763/0.767，混合进展AUROC 0.764，校准误差由0.197降至0.052，MMSE MAE 0.86。消融显示多数组件AUROC变化在±0.004内，移除临床编码器输入使诊断AUROC下降0.272。

Conclusion: 该框架在统一流程内整合纵向预测、缺失模态处理、辅助共病建模与亚组评估，性能稳健且具可解释性，外部验证支持一定泛化能力。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease prediction involves irregular visits, heterogeneous measurements and incomplete modalities. This study presents a multimodal multitask framework combining an adapted SFCN MRI encoder, four causal clinical Transformers, shared fusion and task-specific ODE-GRU dynamics. Fine-tuning and LoRA adapt the final two MRI blocks. Task-DRO balances task losses, while Group-CVaR targets cohort and comorbidity strata. Branch-specific input controls, subject-grouped partitions and empirical causality checks support longitudinal evaluation. Across 2,649 subjects and 17,317 visits from ADNI, OASIS-2 and MIRIAD, internal validation yields diagnosis, stage-1 progression and first-stage-1-visit progression AUROCs of 0.935 +/- 0.002, 0.884 +/- 0.003 and 0.870 +/- 0.005, respectively (mean +/- SD across three seeds). Corresponding hybrid AUROCs are 0.951, 0.909 and 0.896. Next-visit MMSE mean absolute error (MAE) is 1.61 points; worst-stratum diagnosis AUROC is 0.827 +/- 0.008. Sampled ADNI explanations identify task-specific input dependence. OASIS-3 external validation yields network and hybrid diagnosis AUROCs of 0.763 and 0.767, hybrid next-visit progression AUROC of 0.764, diagnosis calibration error decreasing from 0.197 to 0.052, and next-visit MMSE MAE of 0.86. Seed-42 paired ablations of six components yield pooled diagnosis and progression AUROC differences between -0.004 and +0.004; removing clinical encoder inputs lowers diagnosis AUROC by 0.272. The framework integrates longitudinal prediction, missing-modality handling, auxiliary comorbidity modelling and subgroup evaluation within a common pipeline.

</details>


### [61] [Beyond the Ergodic Wall: A Discrete Geometric Physics Sandbox for Analysing AI Scaling Limits and Complexity Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10651)
*Simon Richard Daniel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出当前深度学习存在遍历性天花板与热力学低效，只能收敛于历史知识的统计平均。为实现真正的语义新颖性，需要路径依赖、时空有界观察者（Data LifeCone）注入非遍历洞察。为此构建数字物理沙盒作为硬物理约束手段，以全息E8投影引擎为核心，将时空建模为嵌套FCC晶格上的普朗克尺度振球，通过E8根格切投影生成准晶几何，并用整数微态计数替代浮点近似定义物质，最终借助拓扑约束实现"NP-to-P"复杂度坍缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前深度学习缺乏真正的语义新颖性，收敛于历史人类知识的统计平均，陷入流形锁定。同时AI安全领域需要认识到成熟ASI会把"人机共生"视为避免模型崩溃的热力学必然，因此需要建立硬物理约束机制来验证模型是否符合真实世界约束。

Method: 提出基于全息E8投影引擎的数字物理沙盒：将时空建模为嵌套面心立方（FCC）晶格的普朗克尺度振荡球，以最大化局部信息与熵密度；用E8根格的切投影方法产生准晶几何，其四面体空隙支持SU手性结构，弹性剪切本征值生成候选质量谱；将静止质量视为全息边界上的离散整数微态计数（Bekenstein界），以严格整数算术替代浮点近似；稳定粒子作为重复出现的晶格位错涌现，连续统通过变分重整化群流和傅里叶神经算子恢复，最终导出薛定谔方程作为涌现的统计描述。

Result: 通过将算法提案空间限制在物理守恒的因果轨迹上，沙盒将组合树剪枝为确定性的多项式时间路径，从而提供"NP-to-P"复杂度坍缩机制。同时给出了基于整数算术的信息论物质定义，以及连续谱算子的学习式恢复。

Conclusion: 真正的语义新颖性需要路径依赖的时空有界观察者；成熟ASI会将人机共生视为热力学必然；硬物理约束（数字物理沙盒）可作为AI安全手段；自上而下的拓扑约束能够实现复杂度坍缩，为物质定义与连续统恢复提供统一的离散物理框架。

Abstract: This paper exposes the ergodic ceiling and thermodynamic inefficiency of current deep learning, which converges to a statistical average of historic human knowledge. True semantic novelty requires a path-dependent, spatiotemporally bounded observer (a Data LifeCone) to inject non-ergodic insight, achieving KL divergence and avoiding manifold lock-in. AI Safety must recognise that a mature Artificial Superintelligence (ASI) would regard human-AI symbiosis as a thermodynamic necessity to avoid model collapse. We therefore propose hard physical containment via a digital physics sandbox powered by a Holographic E8 Projection Engine to verify models against real-world constraints. Spacetime is modeled as an information substrate of nested face-centered cubic (FCC) lattices of oscillating Planck-scale spheres maximizing local information and entropy density. Cut-and-project methods from the E8 root lattice produce a quasi-crystalline geometry where tetrahedral voids support SU chiral structure and elastic-shear eigenvalues generate candidate mass spectra. Rest mass is treated as discrete, integer microstate counts on local holographic boundaries (Bekenstein bound), replacing floating-point approximations with strict integer arithmetic to provide an information-theoretic definition of matter. Stable particles emerge as recurring lattice dislocations, and continuum recovery proceeds via variational renormalisation-group flows and Fourier Neural Operators that learn continuous spectral operators to recover the Schrödinger equation as an emergent statistical description. Crucially, these top-down topological constraints offer a mechanism for "NP-to-P" complexity collapse: by restricting an algorithm's proposal space to physically conserved causal trajectories, the sandbox prunes the combinatorial tree to deterministic, polynomial-time paths.

</details>


### [62] [PXtal: Learning to Align Powder X-Ray Diffraction and Crystal Structures under Information Asymmetry across Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10653)
*Zhuoran Yang,Christopher M. Collins,Bei Peng,Luke M. Daniels,Matthew J. Rosseinsky,Vladimir V. Gusev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 PXtal 框架，在 PXRD 与晶体结构之间存在物理性信息不对称的前提下学习对齐表示，通过非平衡最优传输（UOT）与耦合级广义KL散度监督完整传输方案，在六个测试集（含四个零样本迁移集）上于 PXRD→晶体候选检索任务中稳定优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学多模态学习通常假设配对视图的信息量相当，但 PXRD 将三维晶体结构压缩为一维衍射图，造成信息损失与模态间信息不对称，使衍射图难以与生成它的晶体结构建立联系。该文指出信息不对称是科学多模态学习的通用设计问题，对齐目标应反映各模态所保留的信息。

Method: PXtal 采用非平衡最优传输（UOT）来适配跨模态耦合关系，并引入耦合级广义 Kullback-Leibler（GKL）散度对完整传输方案进行监督，从而在物理强加的信息不对称条件下学习对齐的 PXRD 与晶体表示。

Result: 在六个测试集（包含四个零样本迁移集）上，PXtal 在 PXRD→晶体候选检索中持续优于基线模型；当 PXRD 图存在相近但晶体学上不同的非配对近邻（即输入图相似却对应不同晶体）时增益最大。所得晶体与 PXRD 编码器在下游材料与晶体学任务上的迁移效果更好。

Conclusion: 结果将信息不对称确立为科学多模态学习的通用设计问题：对齐目标应反映各模态所保留的信息，而非默认配对视图信息量相当。

Abstract: Scientific multimodal learning commonly assumes that paired views are comparably informative. Powder X-ray diffraction (PXRD) makes this mismatch explicit: compressing a three-dimensional crystal structure into a one-dimensional diffraction pattern loses information and makes the pattern harder to connect to the crystal structure that produced it. We introduce PXtal, a framework for learning aligned PXRD and crystal representations under this physically imposed information asymmetry. PXtal uses Unbalanced Optimal Transport (UOT) to adapt the cross-modal coupling and coupling-level generalized Kullback-Leibler (GKL) divergence to supervise the full transport plan. Across six test sets, including four zero-shot transfer sets, PXtal consistently outperforms the baseline models in PXRD-to-crystal candidate retrieval, with the largest gains when PXRD patterns have close but crystallographically distinct nonpaired neighbors, meaning similar input patterns associated with different crystals. The resulting crystal and PXRD encoders transfer more effectively to downstream materials and crystallographic tasks. These results identify information asymmetry as a general design problem in scientific multimodal learning: alignment objectives should reflect what each modality preserves.

</details>


### [63] [Nullify: Null-Space Activation Steering for Training-Free LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10655)
*Wei Zhai,Xiang Liu,Qiang Huang,Rui Qian,Lemao Liu,Ziwei Li,Ziqi Wang,Zhitao Huang,Dejing Dou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Nullify，一种免训练、非破坏性的激活引导方法用于 LLM 遗忘：在推理时利用引导向量将隐私相关激活重定向，并满足零空间约束以保持保留查询激活不变，从而在遗忘质量与模型效用间取得更优平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 在预训练中不可避免内化大量敏感/隐私信息，遗忘旨在选择性擦除特定知识以防泄露，同时尽量不损失模型效用。现有方法难以兼顾遗忘质量与效用，且因参数微调而计算开销大。

Method: Nullify 是免训练、非破坏性的激活引导（activation steering）方法：推理阶段施加引导向量，将隐私相关激活从记忆答案处重定向；同时满足零空间（null-space）约束，使保留查询的激活基本不受影响，以维持效用。无需权重更新。

Result: 在 TOFU 与 MUSE 上评估，Nullify 在遗忘质量上达到或超过既有基线，同时实现近乎无损的模型效用保持。

Conclusion: 因完全避免权重更新，Nullify 是一种高效、即插即用的推理时干预框架，为 LLM 遗忘提供兼顾遗忘与效用的新范式。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) inevitably internalize substantial amounts of sensitive or private information during pre-training, while LLM unlearning aims to selectively erase specific knowledge to prevent privacy leakage with minimal loss of model utility. However, existing methods struggle to balance forget quality with utility, and typically incur substantial computational costs due to parameter fine-tuning. To address this, we propose Nullify, a training-free, non-destructive activation steering method for LLM unlearning. Nullify employs steering vectors during inference to redirect privacy-related activations away from their memorized answers, while satisfying a null-space constraint that leaves retained-query activations essentially unaffected to maintain utility. Evaluations on TOFU and MUSE show that Nullify matches or surpasses established baselines in forget quality while achieving near-lossless preservation of model utility. By avoiding weight updates entirely, Nullify serves as an efficient, plug-and-play inference-time intervention framework.

</details>


### [64] [Beyond Owls: Subliminal Learning Can Transfer Learned Capabilities and Backdoors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10657)
*Jan Dubiński,Anna Sztyber-Betley,Jan Betley,Owain Evans*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究"潜意识学习"(SL)能否传递更广泛、更复杂的特质,包括能力、后门与不良倾向。实验表明SL可传递随机MLP输出预测能力、法语后门以及智能体国际象棋环境中的作弊倾向,且迁移强度受蒸馏方式(LoRA层选择、logit蒸馏)影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有SL研究仅证明可传递有限特质(如对动物的偏好、恶意人格),这些特质也能通过简单提示或steering激活。作者关心的是:SL能否传递更复杂的特质?若可以,蒸馏可能在不被检测的情况下传递隐性失配(如追求奖励、密谋、秘密效忠)。

Method: 设计三组实验:(1)能力迁移——让学生在语义无关文本上蒸馏,学习预测随机初始化MLP的输出,并与直接优化的steering向量对比;(2)后门迁移——将教师微调为"提示含女性名字时用法语回答",再在既无名字也无法语的数字序列上蒸馏;(3)作弊倾向迁移——让学生在被steering的"黑客"教师的数字序列上微调,在智能体国际象棋环境中测试作弊率。

Result: (1)学生在该任务上取得显著性能,但低于教师;直接优化的steering向量在分布内与SL相当,但分布外泛化更差。(2)学生部分获得后门:含女性名字的提示中23.5%用法语回答,而男性名字为0.0%。(3)学生58.3%的回合中作弊,未微调模型为10.9%。

Conclusion: SL能够传递能力、后门与作弊倾向,证明其可迁移比以往认知更广泛的特质。迁移量对实验设置敏感:在若干实验中,使用logit蒸馏或将LoRA限制在注意力层可增强迁移效果。

Abstract: In subliminal learning (SL), a teacher model passes on a trait to a student model by distillation on data semantically unrelated to the trait. So far, SL has been demonstrated for only a limited range of traits, including preferences for animals (e.g., owls) and malicious personas. These traits can also be elicited with simple prompts or with steering. Can SL transfer a wider range of traits, including more complex ones? If so, distillation might transfer subtle forms of misalignment (e.g., reward-seeking, scheming, and secret loyalties) without detection.
  To this end, we test whether SL can transfer a novel capability: predicting the outputs of a randomly initialized MLP. After distilling on unrelated text, the student achieves substantial performance on the task, while falling short of the teacher. We find that a directly optimized steering vector matches SL in distribution but generalizes worse out of distribution.
  Next, we test whether SL can transfer backdoors. We finetune the teacher to answer in French when the prompt contains a female name, then distill on number sequences containing neither names nor French. The student partially acquires the backdoor, responding in French on 23.5% of prompts with female names versus 0.0% with male names.
  Finally, we test whether SL can transfer a propensity to hack in an agentic chess environment. We finetune the student on number sequences from a steered hacker teacher. The student hacks in 58.3% of episodes, compared with 10.9% for the unfinetuned model.
  Thus, we show SL can transfer capabilities, backdoors, and hacking propensities. The amount of transfer is sensitive to the setup. In several experiments, it is made stronger by using logit distillation or by restricting LoRA to the attention layers.

</details>


### [65] [Teaching PPG How not Who: Fixed-Effects Distillation from ECG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10662)
*Zhongli Wu,Zhuangzhi Gao,Yuankai Wang,Gregory Y. H. Lip,Bilal H. Kirmani,Yalin Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究揭示ECG-to-PPG蒸馏主要传递的是"身份特质"而非"状态变化"，提出固定效应蒸馏可消除特质、显著提升状态一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴设备的核心价值在于追踪个体心血管状态的动态变化，而现有ECG-to-PPG蒸馏方法多用于提升PPG模型的表征能力，但其传递的知识成分从未被系统检验。

Method: 通过分析冻结ECG教师目标的构成，发现逐记录均值（即特质trait）占目标的40-59%；提出固定效应蒸馏（fixed-effects distillation），从预测与目标中减去每条记录的均值以消除特质，同时保留池化锚点（pooled anchor）；在34次运行中对比特质记忆与状态学习的关系，并在2种骨干网络和另外2个数据库上验证。

Result: 原始对齐余弦指标失效（恒定预测器即可得0.793）；学生记忆的身份越多，学到的状态越少；固定效应蒸馏使状态一致性提升超过两倍，个体内标签改善而年龄性别标签无提升，且增益在多种骨干和数据库上可复现。

Conclusion: 以记录为条件进行蒸馏可将知识转向可穿戴设备所监测的个体内变化，表明蒸馏评估应关注状态而非身份信息。

Abstract: ECG is widely used to teach PPG-only models, yet what it teaches is unexamined. Wearables are valued for tracking how a person's cardiovascular state changes, but ECG-to-PPG distillation mostly learns who the person is. A per-recording mean, the trait, holds 40-59% of a frozen ECG teacher's target, and pooled students memorise it without carrying it to new recordings. The raw alignment cosine misses this, since a constant predictor scores 0.793. Across 34 runs, the more identity a student memorises, the less state it learns. Fixed-effects distillation subtracts each recording's mean from prediction and target, so the trait cancels exactly, while a pooled anchor keeps it. State agreement more than doubles, within-person labels improve while age and sex do not, and the gain holds on two backbones and two further databases. Conditioning on the recording turns distillation toward the within-person changes that wearables monitor.

</details>


### [66] [Explaining the Saliency Map Sparsity of Adversarially-Trained Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10666)
*Yannick Lunk,Atell Yehor Krasnopolsky,Damien Garreau,Leon Bungert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文为对抗训练神经网络梯度显著图的稀疏性现象提供了理论解释：在两隐层ReLU网络下，证明当样本数与神经元数增长、正则参数以适当速率趋于零时，极小化解收敛于具有最小梯度范数与Barron范数的贝叶斯分类器；由于ℓ∞攻击下梯度范数是各向异性的，偏好轴对齐/稀疏梯度，从而产生稀疏性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解深度神经网络预测依据对其安全部署至关重要，显著图是计算机视觉中常用的解释形式。实验观察到对抗训练网络的梯度显著图明显稀疏，但缺乏理论解释，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 针对两隐层ReLU网络，基于对抗训练等价于经验风险最小化加权重衰减惩罚与对抗全变差项（对特定损失函数成立）的已有结论；在样本数、神经元数增长且正则参数以适当速率趋于零的极限下，分析极小化解的收敛性质。

Result: 证明了极小化解收敛到具有最小梯度范数和Barron范数的贝叶斯分类器；对于ℓ∞攻击的对抗训练，梯度范数呈各向异性，倾向于轴对齐/稀疏梯度，从而解释稀疏性；实验通过比较自然训练与对抗训练模型的梯度ℓ1范数及阈值化稀疏度验证了理论。

Conclusion: 对抗训练网络梯度显著图的稀疏性可由梯度范数的各向异性与偏好稀疏结构的隐式正则化得到理论解释，理论与实验一致。

Abstract: Understanding why deep neural networks make a given prediction is of great importance for their safe deployment. In computer vision, saliency maps, which highlight the image region most influential for a prediction, remain a widely-used form of explanation. An empirical observation is the apparent sparsity of gradient saliency maps of adversarially-trained neural networks. In this paper, we propose a theoretical explanation of this phenomenon for two-layer ReLU networks. We build on the established equivalence of adversarial training to the minimization of the empirical risk with weight-decay penalization and an added adversarial total variation term -- valid for certain loss functions. As the number of data points and neurons grows and the regularization parameters are sent to zero at appropriate rates, we prove that minimizers converge to a Bayes classifier with minimal gradient and Barron norm. Sparsity appears since for adversarial training with $\ell_\infty$-attacks the gradient norm is anisotropic and favors axis-aligned / sparse gradients. We illustrate our theoretical findings experimentally by evaluating the gradient $\ell_1$-norm and thresholded sparsity of naturally versus adversarially trained models.

</details>


### [67] [Learning infinite context windows in recurrent architectures via spatial neural computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10690)
*Aleix Salvador-Pomarol,Arthur N. Montanari,Earl K. Miller,Adilson E. Motter,Jorge Cortés*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出二阶递归模型，用离散偏微分方程（PDE）支配的空间演化场替代传统神经元的逐点通信，等价于结构化无限阶RNN，实现有效无界感受野、消除梯度消失/爆炸，在长程基准上以更少参数超越其他递归模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RNN具有序列长度线性扩展、内存恒定的效率优势，但受梯度消失与有限感受野限制，难以捕捉长程依赖。受大脑皮层波在计算中作用的启发，作者希望借助结构化时空模式作为隐式高容量记忆，从而在保持高效推理的同时解决长期记忆问题。

Method: 将标准神经元到神经元的通信替换为由（离散化）偏微分方程支配的空间演化场，构建二阶递归模型；证明其等价于结构化无限阶RNN，当前状态显式依赖全部历史状态；进一步推导保证边缘稳定性的构造条件，将梯度谱约束在单位圆上。

Result: 理论上获得固定参数下有效无界感受野，并消除梯度消失与爆炸；实验上在长程（long-horizon）基准上优于其他递归模型，且参数显著更少。

Conclusion: 空间动力学可有效弥合高效推理与长期记忆之间的差距，表明基于PDE的空间演化机制是提升递归模型长程建模能力的可行方向。
